/bin/bash -lc 'nice ionice -c 3 nl -ba prototypes/chip-top/src/chip_spec.ml' in /home/matthias/prog/janestreet/emulator-wt-chip-assists
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	(* Executable specification of the whole chip core, one clock per [step].
     2	
     3	   It is composed from the blocks' own models, which are not written anew here:
     4	   - the sequencer: ISA v2's interpreter (Isa2, ../sequencer-v2/isa2.ml);
     5	   - the PE array: Upe.Model (../unified-pe/verify/model.ml);
     6	   - the streamer and the sampler: Pstream.Model and Psample.Model;
     7	   - the edge-tracking sampler and the CRC unit: the models in Eth10.Edge_sampler and
     8	     Eth10.Crc_unit; the matcher: the closed form of Eth10.Model;
     9	   - the bit-path assists: Txpath (the transmit serialiser) and Rxpath (the receive framer) of
    10	     ../bit-assists, built from its stuff tracker and line coder.
    11	   New here, because nothing specified them before: the glue (host link, its FIFOs, the register
    12	   file, the pin map and pad sources, the flag selects, the port holds between the threads and
    13	   the array, the memories) and the pin NCO, which has RTL (../multiphase/nco.ml) but no model.
    14	   The NCO's behaviour is written below from nco.ml's header comment.
    15	
    16	   The boundary is the four-phase stage's (README, "Phases"): inputs are, per input pad, the
    17	   four quarter samples the core sees in this clock (bit p = quarter p); outputs are, per output
    18	   pad, the nibble of levels for the next clock's four quarters, and the uio output enables.
    19	   Outputs depend only on the state at the start of the clock.
    20	
    21	   Within a clock everything reads the state as it was at the start of the clock, and every
    22	   update lands at its end, as registers do. Where models must be stepped in some order, the
    23	   order below is the one in which their inputs become available, and no model reads another's
    24	   updated state. *)
    25	
    26	open Regs
    27	module UM = Upe.Model
    28	module US = Upe.Spec
    29	module SM = Pstream.Model
    30	module PM = Psample.Model
    31	module ES = Eth10.Edge_sampler
    32	module CRC = Eth10.Crc_unit
    33	module MM = Eth10.Model
    34	module TX = Txpath
    35	module RX = Rxpath
    36	
    37	type config = { layout : US.layout; prog_words : int }
    38	
    39	let default_config = { layout = US.layout_of_sizes [| 1; 1; 1; 1 |]; prog_words = 1024 }
    40	let bank_bytes = Isa2.bank_len
    41	let hostin_depth = 16 and hostout_depth = 8
    42	
    43	(* a host-link byte that was decoded in the previous clock and acts in this one *)
    44	type action =
    45	  | A_reg of int * int            (* address, byte *)
    46	  | A_prog of int * int
    47	  | A_bank of int * int
    48	  | A_hostin of int
    49	  | A_pecfg of int * int          (* segment, byte *)
    50	  | A_peinit of int * int
    51	  | A_peseg of int * int * int    (* segment, sel, byte *)
    52	  | A_stream of int * int         (* address mod 4, byte *)
    53	  | A_match of int                (* bit *)
    54	
    55	type parser = Idle | Addr_hi | Addr_lo | Count | Wdata | Rdata
    56	
    57	type t = {
    58	  cfg : config;
    59	  regs : int array;
    60	  mutable err_mem : bool; mutable hin_ovf : bool; mutable hout_ovf : bool; mutable act_drop : bool;
    61	  prog : int array;
    62	  bank : int array;
    63	  mutable seq : Isa2.state;
    64	  hostin : int Queue.t;
    65	  hostout : (int * int) Queue.t;
    66	  mutable out_pend : (int * int) option;     (* the core's OUT, registered; enters the FIFO now *)
    67	  mutable push_pend : (int * int) option;    (* the core's SEND to a port, registered *)
    68	  send_hi : bool array;                      (* per port: the next SEND byte is the high byte *)
    69	  hold_st : int array;                       (* per port: 0 empty, 1 low byte next, 2 high byte next *)
    70	  hold_w : int array;
    71	  mutable pe : UM.t;
    72	  mutable str : SM.t;
    73	  mutable smp : PM.t;
    74	  mutable es : ES.st;
    75	  mutable es_out : ES.out;
    76	  mutable es_last : int;                     (* the last recovered bit, held between bits *)
    77	  mutable crc : int;
    78	  chain : int array;                         (* the matcher's 37 configuration bits, in chain order *)
    79	  mutable mhist : int array;
    80	  mutable mtime : int;
    81	  mutable nco_acc : int;
    82	  mutable nco_out : int;
    83	  mutable tx : TX.st;
    84	  mutable rx : RX.st;
    85	  (* host link *)
    86	  mutable prev_s : int; mutable prev_r : int;
    87	  mutable nphase : int; mutable hi_nib : int;
    88	  mutable pst : parser; mutable op : int; mutable tgt : int; mutable addr : int;
    89	  mutable remaining : int;
    90	  mutable rbuf : int; mutable rphase : int;
    91	  mutable pend_action : action option;
    92	  mutable pend_fetch : (int * int) option;   (* target, address *)
    93	  mutable prog_lo : int; mutable str_lo : int; mutable str_hi : int;
    94	  mutable hout_lat : bool; mutable smp_lat : bool;
    95	  mutable last_fetch : (int * int * int) option;   (* for diagnostics *)
    96	}
    97	
    98	let rep4 b = if b <> 0 then 15 else 0
    99	let bit v i = (v lsr i) land 1
   100	let u32 v = v land 0xFFFF_FFFF
   101	let reg32 t a = t.regs.(a) lor (t.regs.(a + 1) lsl 8) lor (t.regs.(a + 2) lsl 16) lor (t.regs.(a + 3) lsl 24)
   102	
   103	(* ---- block configurations from the registers ---- *)
   104	
   105	let streamer_cfg t : SM.cfg =
   106	  let r = t.regs in
   107	  { period = r.(r_str_per) lor ((r.(r_str_per + 1) land 15) lsl 8); width = (r.(r_str_per + 1) lsr 4) land 7;
   108	    od_mask = r.(r_str_pins) land 15; idle_out = (r.(r_str_pins) lsr 4) land 15; idle_oe = r.(r_str_idle_oe) land 15 }
   109	
   110	let sampler_cfg t : PM.cfg =
   111	  let r = t.regs in
   112	  { clocked = bit r.(r_smp_per + 1) 7 = 1; period = r.(r_smp_per) lor ((r.(r_smp_per + 1) land 15) lsl 8);
   113	    width = (r.(r_smp_per + 1) lsr 4) land 7; trig_pin = (r.(r_smp_off + 1) lsr 4) land 3;
   114	    trig_val = bit r.(r_smp_off + 1) 6; offset = r.(r_smp_off) lor ((r.(r_smp_off + 1) land 15) lsl 8);
   115	    frame_len = r.(r_smp_frame) }
   116	
   117	let es_cfg t : ES.cfg =
   118	  let r = t.regs in
   119	  { mode = (match r.(r_es_mode) land 3 with 0 -> ES.Manchester | 2 -> ES.Nrz | _ -> ES.Biphase_mark);
   120	    invert = bit r.(r_es_mode) 2 = 1;
   121	    holdoff = r.(r_es_holdoff) lor ((r.(r_es_holdoff + 1) land 3) lsl 8);
   122	    timeout = r.(r_es_timeout) lor ((r.(r_es_timeout + 1) land 3) lsl 8);
   123	    offset = r.(r_es_offset); period = r.(r_es_period) }
   124	
   125	(* The CRC unit's model takes a width; the RTL takes a mask. The host must write a mask of the
   126	   form 2^w - 1 (README); the width is then the number of ones. *)
   127	let crc_cfg t : CRC.cfg =
   128	  let mask = reg32 t r_crc_mask in
   129	  let rec ones v = if v = 0 then 0 else (v land 1) + ones (v lsr 1) in
   130	  { width = max 1 (ones mask); poly = reg32 t r_crc_poly; init = reg32 t r_crc_init;
   131	    reflected = bit t.regs.(r_crc_ctrl) 0 = 1; xorout = reg32 t r_crc_xorout; residue = reg32 t r_crc_residue }
   132	
   133	let matcher_cfg t : MM.cfg =
   134	  { t = Array.init MM.n (fun i -> t.chain.(2 * i + 1)); m = Array.init MM.n (fun i -> t.chain.(2 * i));
   135	    thr = Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 5 (fun k -> t.chain.(32 + k) lsl k)) }
   136	
   137	let matcher_now t =
   138	  let c = matcher_cfg t and x k = t.mhist.(k) in
   139	  (MM.y_at c x t.mtime, MM.hit_at c x t.mtime)
   140	
   141	(* the bit-path assists' configurations *)
   142	let code_cfg v : Linecode.cfg = { mode = Linecode.mode_of_code (v land 3); invert = bit v 2 = 1; toggle_on = bit v 3 }
   143	let stuff_cfg v : Stuff.cfg = { run = v land 15; either = bit v 4 = 1; value = bit v 5 }
   144	
   145	let tx_cfg t : TX.cfg =
   146	  let r = t.regs in
   147	  let tail v : TX.tail = { len = v land 15; la = bit v 4; lb = bit v 5 } in
   148	  { period = r.(r_tx_per) lor ((r.(r_tx_per + 1) land 15) lsl 8); msb_first = bit r.(r_tx_code) 4 = 1;
   149	    stuff = stuff_cfg r.(r_tx_stuff); code = code_cfg r.(r_tx_code); tail1 = tail r.(r_tx_tail1); tail2 = tail r.(r_tx_tail2) }
   150	
   151	let rx_cfg t : RX.cfg =
   152	  let r = t.regs and c = t.regs.(r_bp_ctrl) in
   153	  { code = code_cfg r.(r_rx_code); stuff = stuff_cfg r.(r_rx_stuff); msb_first = bit r.(r_rx_code) 4 = 1;
   154	    pattern = r.(r_rx_pattern); pmask = r.(r_rx_pmask); start_on_pattern = bit c 7 = 1; half_duplex = bit c 4 = 1;
   155	    end_inactive = bit c 5 = 1; end_burst = bit c 6 = 1; skip = r.(r_rx_skip) }
   156	
   157	(* the MBX port that is the bit path's, if one is *)
   158	let bp_port t = let c = t.regs.(r_bp_ctrl) in if bit c 0 = 1 then Some ((c lsr 1) land 3) else None
   159	let crc_from_frame t = bit t.regs.(r_bp_ctrl) 3 = 1
   160	
   161	(* the edge sampler's active input, from this clock's pads *)
   162	let line_active t ~smp =
   163	  let lvl i = bit smp.(i) 3 in
   164	  let m = t.regs.(r_es_mode) and es_pad = t.regs.(r_es_pads) land 15 and act_pad = (t.regs.(r_es_pads) lsr 4) land 15 in
   165	  bit m 3 = 0 || lvl act_pad = 1 || (bit m 4 = 1 && lvl es_pad = 1)
   166	
   167	let boot t = Array.init 4 (fun th -> ((t.regs.(r_boot_page) lsr (2 * th)) land 3, t.regs.(r_boot_pc th)))
   168	
   169	(* coverage of chip-level events, for the lockstep's report (not part of the behaviour) *)
   170	let cov : (string, int) Hashtbl.t = Hashtbl.create 64
   171	let hit name = Hashtbl.replace cov name (1 + Option.value ~default:0 (Hashtbl.find_opt cov name))
   172	
   173	let es_zero : ES.out = { bit = 0; valid = false; burst_end = false; overrun = false }
   174	
   175	(* The edge sampler's bit output is defined only while its valid is high; the glue holds the
   176	   last recovered bit, so pads, flags and the sampler see a level. *)
   177	let es_level t = if t.es_out.valid then t.es_out.bit else t.es_last
   178	
   179	let create ?(cfg = default_config) () =
   180	  let regs = Array.init reg_space reset_value in
   181	  let t =
   182	    { cfg; regs; err_mem = false; hin_ovf = false; hout_ovf = false; act_drop = false;
   183	      prog = Array.make cfg.prog_words 0; bank = Array.make bank_bytes 0;
   184	      seq = Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:(Array.make 4 (0, 0)) ~bank:(Array.make bank_bytes 0);
   185	      hostin = Queue.create (); hostout = Queue.create (); out_pend = None; push_pend = None;
   186	      send_hi = Array.make 4 false; hold_st = Array.make 4 0; hold_w = Array.make 4 0;
   187	      pe = UM.create ~layout:cfg.layout ();
   188	      str = SM.create { period = 1; width = 1; od_mask = 0; idle_out = 0; idle_oe = 0 };
   189	      smp = PM.create { clocked = false; period = 1; width = 1; trig_pin = 0; trig_val = 0; offset = 0; frame_len = 0 };
   190	      es = ES.init (); es_out = es_zero; es_last = 0; crc = 0; chain = Array.make 37 0; mhist = Array.make 1024 0;
   191	      mtime = 0; nco_acc = 0; nco_out = 0; tx = TX.init (); rx = RX.init (); prev_s = 0; prev_r = 0; nphase = 0; hi_nib = 0; pst = Idle;
   192	      op = 0; tgt = 0; addr = 0; remaining = 0; rbuf = 0; rphase = 0; pend_action = None;
   193	      pend_fetch = None; prog_lo = 0; str_lo = 0; str_hi = 0; hout_lat = false; smp_lat = false; last_fetch = None }
   194	  in
   195	  t.seq <- Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:(boot t) ~bank:t.bank;
   196	  t.str <- SM.create (streamer_cfg t);
   197	  t.smp <- PM.create (sampler_cfg t);
   198	  t
   199	
   200	(* ---- outputs: from the state at the start of the clock ---- *)
   201	
   202	type outputs = { pad_nib : int array; uio_oe : int }
   203	
   204	let taps t = (UM.state t.pe).taps
   205	
   206	let rec source t (taps : US.tap array) src =
   207	  if src < 8 then ((t.seq.pin_sub lsr (4 * src)) land 15, bit t.seq.pin_oe src)
   208	  else if src < 12 then (rep4 (bit t.str.out (src - 8)), bit t.str.oe (src - 8))
   209	  else if src = src_nco_quarter then (t.nco_out land 15, 1)
   210	  else if src = src_nco_half then ((t.nco_out lsr 4) land 15, 1)
   211	  else if src = src_nco_grid then ((t.nco_out lsr 8) land 15, 1)
   212	  else if src >= 16 && src < 20 then (rep4 (Bool.to_int taps.(src - 16).tf), 1)
   213	  else if src >= 20 && src < 24 then (rep4 (taps.(src - 20).td land 1), 1)
   214	  else if src = src_es_bit then (rep4 (es_level t), 1)
   215	  else if src = src_tx_a || src = src_tx_b then begin
   216	    if t.tx.busy then (rep4 (if src = src_tx_a then t.tx.a else t.tx.b), 1)
   217	    else source t taps ((t.regs.(r_tx_idle) lsr (if src = src_tx_a then 0 else 4)) land 7)
   218	  end
   219	  else (0, 1)
   220	
   221	let outputs t =
   222	  let taps = taps t in
   223	  let pad_nib = Array.make n_pads 0 and uio_oe = ref 0 in
   224	  List.iter
   225	    (fun p ->
   226	      let sel = t.regs.(r_padsel p) in
   227	      let nib, oe = source t taps (sel land 31) in
   228	      let nib = if bit sel 5 = 1 then nib lxor 15 else nib in
   229	      if p < 8 then pad_nib.(p) <- (if oe = 1 then nib else rep4 (bit sel 6))
   230	      else begin
   231	        pad_nib.(p) <- nib;
   232	        if oe = 1 then uio_oe := !uio_oe lor (1 lsl (p - 8))
   233	      end)
   234	    general_out_pads;
   235	  (* host data: the current read nibble, driven while the host's read request is seen *)
   236	  let rnib = if t.rphase = 0 then t.rbuf lsr 4 else t.rbuf land 15 in
   237	  for k = 0 to 3 do pad_nib.(pad_hdata + k) <- rep4 (bit rnib k) done;
   238	  if t.prev_r = 1 then uio_oe := !uio_oe lor 0xF;
   239	  { pad_nib; uio_oe = !uio_oe }
   240	
   241	(* [tx_pend]: a SEND to the bit path's port is on its way (registered in the core) *)
   242	let tx_active t = TX.active t.tx || (match t.push_pend, bp_port t with Some (p, _), Some q -> p = q | _ -> false)
   243	
   244	let flags t (taps : US.tap array) ~line_act =
   245	  let src s =
   246	    if s < 4 then taps.(s).tf
   247	    else if s < 8 then taps.(s - 4).tv
   248	    else if s = fl_es_valid then t.es_out.valid
   249	    else if s = fl_es_bit then es_level t = 1
   250	    else if s = fl_es_in_burst then t.es.in_burst
   251	    else if s = fl_es_burst_end then t.es_out.burst_end
   252	    else if s = fl_crc_check then t.crc = (crc_cfg t).residue
   253	    else if s = fl_match_hit then snd (matcher_now t)
   254	    else if s = fl_stream_full then SM.full t.str
   255	    else if s = fl_sample_valid then not (Queue.is_empty t.smp.fifo)
   256	    else if s = fl_nco then bit t.nco_out 0 = 1
   257	    else if s = fl_rx_done then RX.done_ t.rx
   258	    else if s = fl_rx_err then t.rx.err
   259	    else if s = fl_rx_avail then not (Queue.is_empty t.rx.fifo)
   260	    else if s = fl_tx_active then tx_active t
   261	    else if s = fl_line_active then line_act
   262	    else if s = fl_line_inactive then not line_act
   263	    else if s = fl_rx_in_frame then t.rx.in_frame
   264	    else false
   265	  in
   266	  let v = ref 0 in
   267	  for f = 0 to 15 do if src (t.regs.(r_flagsel f) land 31) then v := !v lor (1 lsl f) done;
   268	  !v
   269	
   270	(* ---- host-link reads ---- *)
   271	
   272	let run t = bit t.regs.(r_ctrl) 0 = 1
   273	let held t = bit t.regs.(r_ctrl) 1 = 1
   274	
   275	let status t =
   276	  Bool.to_int t.err_mem lor (Bool.to_int t.hin_ovf lsl 1) lor (Bool.to_int t.hout_ovf lsl 2)
   277	  lor (Bool.to_int (Queue.length t.hostin >= hostin_depth) lsl 3)
   278	  lor (Bool.to_int (not (Queue.is_empty t.hostout)) lsl 4)
   279	  lor (Bool.to_int (not (Queue.is_empty t.smp.fifo)) lsl 5)
   280	  lor (Bool.to_int (SM.full t.str) lsl 6) lor (Bool.to_int t.act_drop lsl 7)
   281	
   282	let read_reg t a =
   283	  if a >= reg_space then 0
   284	  else if rw_bits a > 0 then t.regs.(a)
   285	  else if a = r_status then status t
   286	  else if a = r_hostin_count then Queue.length t.hostin
   287	  else if a = r_hostout_count then Queue.length t.hostout
   288	  else if a >= r_crc_raw && a < r_crc_raw + 4 then (t.crc lsr (8 * (a - r_crc_raw))) land 0xFF
   289	  else if a = r_match then (let y, hit = matcher_now t in y lor (Bool.to_int hit lsl 7))
   290	  else if a = r_smp_overflows then t.smp.overflows land 0xFF
   291	  else if a = r_bp_status then
   292	    Bool.to_int t.rx.in_frame lor (Bool.to_int t.rx.err lsl 1) lor (Bool.to_int t.rx.ovf lsl 2)
   293	    lor (Queue.length t.rx.fifo lsl 3) lor (Bool.to_int t.tx.busy lsl 5) lor (Queue.length t.tx.fifo lsl 6)
   294	  else 0
   295	
   296	(* the byte a fetch returns, and whether it pops the sampler *)
   297	let fetch t (tgt, a) =
   298	  let a = a land 0xFFFF in
   299	  if tgt = t_reg then (read_reg t a, false)
   300	  else if tgt = t_prog then
   301	    if run t then (t.err_mem <- true; (0, false))
   302	    else
   303	      let w = t.prog.((a lsr 1) land (t.cfg.prog_words - 1)) in
   304	      ((if a land 1 = 0 then w else w lsr 8) land 0xFF, false)
   305	  else if tgt = t_bank then
   306	    if run t then (t.err_mem <- true; (0, false)) else (t.bank.(a land (bank_bytes - 1)), false)
   307	  else if tgt = t_hostout then
   308	    if a land 1 = 0 then begin
   309	      t.hout_lat <- not (Queue.is_empty t.hostout);
   310	      ((match Queue.peek_opt t.hostout with Some (tag, _) -> 0x80 lor tag | None -> 0), false)
   311	    end
   312	    else if t.hout_lat then (t.hout_lat <- false; (snd (Queue.pop t.hostout), false))
   313	    else begin
   314	      if not (Queue.is_empty t.hostout) then hit "HOSTOUT entry arrived between tag and data";
   315	      (0, false)
   316	    end
   317	  else if tgt = t_sample then
   318	    match a land 3, Queue.peek_opt t.smp.fifo with
   319	    | 0, h -> t.smp_lat <- h <> None; ((match h with Some (_, n) -> 0x80 lor n | None -> 0), false)
   320	    | 1, Some (w, _) when t.smp_lat -> (w land 0xFF, false)
   321	    | 2, h ->
   322	      let lat = t.smp_lat in
   323	      t.smp_lat <- false;
   324	      (match h with Some (w, _) when lat -> ((w lsr 8) land 0xFF, true) | _ -> (0, false))
   325	    | _ -> (0, false)
   326	  else (0, false)
   327	
   328	(* the action of one written data byte *)
   329	let action_of ~tgt ~addr b =
   330	  let a = addr land 0xFFFF in
   331	  if tgt = t_reg then Some (A_reg (a, b))
   332	  else if tgt = t_prog then Some (A_prog (a, b))
   333	  else if tgt = t_bank then Some (A_bank (a, b))
   334	  else if tgt = t_hostin then Some (A_hostin b)
   335	  else if tgt = t_pecfg then Some (A_pecfg ((a lsr 8) land 3, b))
   336	  else if tgt = t_peinit then Some (A_peinit ((a lsr 8) land 3, b))
   337	  else if tgt = t_peseg then Some (A_peseg ((a lsr 2) land 3, a land 3, b))
   338	  else if tgt = t_stream then Some (A_stream (a land 3, b))
   339	  else if tgt = t_match then Some (A_match (b land 1))
   340	  else None
   341	
   342	(* ---- one clock ---- *)
   343	
   344	let reset_all t ~smp =
   345	  (* the core's clear loads the boot registers as they are during this clock *)
   346	  let boot_now = boot t in
   347	  Array.iteri (fun a _ -> t.regs.(a) <- reset_value a) t.regs;
   348	  t.err_mem <- false; t.hin_ovf <- false; t.hout_ovf <- false; t.act_drop <- false;
   349	  Queue.clear t.hostin; Queue.clear t.hostout; t.out_pend <- None; t.push_pend <- None;
   350	  Array.fill t.send_hi 0 4 false; Array.fill t.hold_st 0 4 0; Array.fill t.hold_w 0 4 0;
   351	  t.es <- ES.init (); t.es_out <- es_zero; t.es_last <- 0; t.crc <- 0; t.mtime <- 0; t.nco_acc <- 0; t.nco_out <- 0;
   352	  t.tx <- TX.init (); t.rx <- RX.init ();
   353	  t.prev_s <- bit smp.(pad_hstb) 3; t.prev_r <- bit smp.(pad_hrd) 3;
   354	  t.nphase <- 0; t.hi_nib <- 0; t.pst <- Idle; t.op <- 0; t.tgt <- 0; t.addr <- 0; t.remaining <- 0;
   355	  t.rbuf <- 0; t.rphase <- 0; t.pend_action <- None; t.pend_fetch <- None;
   356	  t.prog_lo <- 0; t.str_lo <- 0; t.str_hi <- 0; t.hout_lat <- false; t.smp_lat <- false;
   357	  t.seq <- Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:boot_now ~bank:t.bank;
   358	  t.str <- SM.create (streamer_cfg t);
   359	  t.smp <- PM.create (sampler_cfg t)
   360	
   361	let step t ~smp ~reset =
   362	  let out = outputs t in
   363	  let taps = taps t in
   364	  if reset then begin
   365	    (* the reset clears the array too: every PE's state and configuration, the feed and control
   366	       registers, as at power-up *)
   367	    t.pe <- UM.create ~layout:t.cfg.layout ();
   368	    reset_all t ~smp;
   369	    out
   370	  end
   371	  else begin
   372	    let lvl i = bit smp.(i) 3 in
   373	    let running = run t and hold = held t and boot_now = boot t in
   374	    let line_act = line_active t ~smp in
   375	    let flags = flags t taps ~line_act in
   376	    (* the bit path's port: RECV reads the framer's FIFO, SEND fills the serialiser's (the SEND
   377	       reaches it a clock later, so a SEND on its way counts as a byte in it) *)
   378	    let bpp = bp_port t in
   379	    let routed p = bpp = Some p in
   380	    let tx_pend = match t.push_pend with Some (p, _) when routed p -> 1 | _ -> 0 in
   381	    let tx_ready = (not hold) && Queue.length t.tx.fifo + tx_pend < TX.depth in
   382	    let act = t.pend_action in
   383	    let reg_act a = match act with Some (A_reg (x, b)) when x = a -> Some b | _ -> None in
   384	    (* the host's fetch, from the state at the start of the clock *)
   385	    let fetched = Option.map (fetch t) t.pend_fetch in
   386	    (match t.pend_fetch, fetched with
   387	     | Some (tg, a), Some (b, _) -> t.last_fetch <- Some (tg, a, b)
   388	     | _ -> ());
   389	    (* sequencer *)
   390	    let pin_sel k = t.regs.(r_pinin k) land 15 in
   391	    let pin_in = Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 8 (fun k -> lvl (pin_sel k) lsl k)) in
   392	    let pin_in4 = Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 8 (fun k -> smp.(pin_sel k) lsl (4 * k))) in
   393	    let new_push = ref None and new_out = ref None and hostin_pop = ref false in
   394	    let port_pops = Array.make 4 false in
   395	    if running then begin
   396	      let host_ctl =
   397	        Option.map (fun b -> (b land 3, (b lsr 2) land 3, t.regs.(r_restart_pc))) (reg_act r_restart) in
   398	      let io =
   399	        Isa2.io ~pin_in4 ~host_in:(Option.value ~default:0 (Queue.peek_opt t.hostin))
   400	          ~host_in_valid:(not (Queue.is_empty t.hostin))
   401	          ~port_in:(Array.init 4 (fun p ->
   402	              if routed p then Option.value ~default:0 (Queue.peek_opt t.rx.fifo)
   403	              else if t.hold_st.(p) = 2 then t.hold_w.(p) lsr 8 else t.hold_w.(p) land 0xFF))
   404	          ~port_in_valid:(Array.init 4 (fun p -> if routed p then not (Queue.is_empty t.rx.fifo) else t.hold_st.(p) <> 0))
   405	          ~port_out_ready:(Array.init 4 (fun p -> if routed p then tx_ready else true)) ~flags
   406	          ?host_ctl pin_in
   407	      in
   408	      let w = t.prog.(Isa2.fetch_addr t.seq t.seq.thread land (t.cfg.prog_words - 1)) in
   409	      hit (Printf.sprintf "op %X" (w lsr 12));
   410	      let e = Isa2.step_f t.seq ~fetch:(fun a -> t.prog.(a land (t.cfg.prog_words - 1))) io in
   411	      if host_ctl <> None then hit "restart";
   412	      if e.host_in_ready then hit "IN from host";
   413	      (match e.port_pop with Some _ -> hit "RECV from a segment" | None -> ());
   414	      (match e.port_push with Some _ -> hit "SEND to a segment" | None -> ());
   415	      (match e.host_out with Some _ -> hit "OUT to host" | None -> ());
   416	      (match e.bank_write with Some _ -> hit "STB" | None -> ());
   417	      if flags <> 0 then hit "a flag input set";
   418	      if e.host_in_ready then hostin_pop := true;
   419	      (match e.port_pop with Some p -> port_pops.(p) <- true | None -> ());
   420	      new_push := e.port_push;
   421	      new_out := e.host_out
   422	    end;
   423	    (* the array: a thread's SEND (registered last clock) goes first; a host write waits *)
   424	    let thread_push = match t.push_pend with Some (p, _) when routed p -> None | x -> x in
   425	    let tx_push = match t.push_pend with Some (p, b) when routed p -> hit "SEND to the serialiser"; Some b | _ -> None in
   426	    let mbx, host_mbx_done =
   427	      match thread_push, act with
   428	      | Some (p, b), _ -> ((true, p, (if t.send_hi.(p) then 1 else 0), b), false)
   429	      | None, Some (A_peseg (sg, sel, b)) -> hit "host feed write"; ((true, sg, sel, b), true)
   430	      | None, _ -> ((false, 0, 0, 0), false)
   431	    in
   432	    let mbx_wr, mbx_seg, mbx_sel, mbx_byte = mbx in
   433	    let cfg_w = match act with Some (A_pecfg (sg, b)) -> Some (sg, b) | _ -> None in
   434	    let init_w = match act with Some (A_peinit (sg, b)) -> Some (sg, b) | _ -> None in
   435	    UM.cycle t.pe
   436	      { mbx_wr; mbx_seg; mbx_sel; mbx_byte;
   437	        cfg_wr = cfg_w <> None; cfg_seg = Option.fold ~none:0 ~some:fst cfg_w; cfg_byte = Option.fold ~none:0 ~some:snd cfg_w;
   438	        init_wr = init_w <> None; init_seg = Option.fold ~none:0 ~some:fst init_w;
   439	        init_byte = Option.fold ~none:0 ~some:snd init_w;
   440	        fixed_d = [| es_level t; 0; 0; 0 |]; fixed_v = [| t.es_out.valid; false; false; false |] };
   441	    (* port glue: SEND byte order, RECV holding registers *)
   442	    let port_reset = Option.value ~default:0 (reg_act r_port_reset) in
   443	    (match thread_push with Some (p, _) -> t.send_hi.(p) <- not t.send_hi.(p) | None -> ());
   444	    let hold_pops = Array.mapi (fun p x -> x && not (routed p)) port_pops in
   445	    let rx_pop = match bpp with Some p -> port_pops.(p) | None -> false in
   446	    if rx_pop then hit "RECV from the framer";
   447	    for p = 0 to 3 do
   448	      if bit port_reset p = 1 then (t.send_hi.(p) <- false; t.hold_st.(p) <- 0)
   449	      else
   450	        match t.hold_st.(p) with
   451	        | 0 -> if taps.(p).tv then (hit "tap captured"; t.hold_st.(p) <- 1; t.hold_w.(p) <- taps.(p).td)
   452	        | 1 -> if hold_pops.(p) then t.hold_st.(p) <- 2
   453	        | _ -> if hold_pops.(p) then t.hold_st.(p) <- 0
   454	    done;
   455	    t.push_pend <- !new_push;
   456	    (* host FIFOs: pops before pushes, so a full FIFO takes a push in the clock its head leaves *)
   457	    if !hostin_pop then ignore (Queue.pop t.hostin);
   458	    (match act with
   459	     | Some (A_hostin b) -> hit "HOSTIN push"; if Queue.length t.hostin < hostin_depth then Queue.push b t.hostin else t.hin_ovf <- true
   460	     | _ -> ());
   461	    (match t.out_pend with
   462	     | Some e -> if Queue.length t.hostout < hostout_depth then Queue.push e t.hostout else t.hout_ovf <- true
   463	     | None -> ());
   464	    t.out_pend <- !new_out;
   465	    (* the assists *)
   466	    let smp_pop = match fetched with Some (_, p) -> p | None -> false in
   467	    let es_old = t.es_out and es_lvl = es_level t and c_residue_now = (crc_cfg t).residue in
   468	    let _, hit_now = matcher_now t in
   469	    if hold then begin
   470	      t.es <- ES.init (); t.es_out <- es_zero; t.es_last <- 0; t.crc <- 0; t.mtime <- 0; t.nco_acc <- 0; t.nco_out <- 0;
   471	      t.tx <- TX.init (); t.rx <- RX.init ()
   472	    end
   473	    else begin
   474	      (* the bit-path assists: the framer on the edge sampler's bits of last clock (as the CRC
   475	         unit and the matcher), the serialiser with the SEND that left the core last clock *)
   476	      let pulse = Option.value ~default:0 (reg_act r_bp_pulse) in
   477	      let rx_in_frame = t.rx.in_frame in
   478	      let rxo =
   479	        RX.step (rx_cfg t) t.rx
   480	          { bit = es_old.bit; valid = es_old.valid; active = line_act; burst_end = es_old.burst_end; start = bit pulse 0 = 1;
   481	            stop = bit pulse 1 = 1; tx_active = TX.active t.tx; pop = rx_pop } in
   482	      if rxo.frame_start then hit "frame start";
   483	      if rx_in_frame && not t.rx.in_frame then hit "frame end";
   484	      if t.tx.busy then hit "serialiser busy";
   485	      ignore (TX.step (tx_cfg t) t.tx ~push:tx_push);
   486	      if t.str.left > 0 || not (Queue.is_empty t.str.fifo) then hit "streamer busy";
   487	      SM.step t.str;
   488	      (match act with
   489	       | Some (A_stream (2, b)) -> SM.push ~count:(b land 15) t.str ((t.str_hi lsl 8) lor t.str_lo)
   490	       | _ -> ());
   491	      let pins =
   492	        if bit t.regs.(r_smp_src) 0 = 1 then es_lvl lor (Bool.to_int es_old.valid lsl 1)
   493	        else Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 4 (fun j -> lvl (t.regs.(r_smp_pin j) land 15) lsl j))
   494	      in
   495	      let before = Queue.length t.smp.fifo in
   496	      ignore (PM.step t.smp ~pins ~pop:smp_pop);
   497	      if Queue.length t.smp.fifo > before then hit "sampler word";
   498	      let es_pad = t.regs.(r_es_pads) land 15 and act_pad = (t.regs.(r_es_pads) lsr 4) land 15 in
   499	      let active = line_act in
   500	      ignore act_pad;
   501	      if es_old.valid then hit "recovered bit";
   502	      if hit_now then hit "matcher hit";
   503	      if t.crc = c_residue_now then hit "CRC check true";
   504	      t.es_last <- es_lvl;
   505	      t.es_out <- ES.step_clock (es_cfg t) t.es ~active (List.init 4 (fun p -> bit smp.(es_pad) p));
   506	      (* the CRC unit's bits: the edge sampler's, or the framer's feed, which also starts it *)
   507	      let cb, cv, fstart = if crc_from_frame t then (rxo.crc_bit, rxo.crc_valid, rxo.frame_start) else (es_old.bit, es_old.valid, false) in
   508	      let start = reg_act r_crc_start <> None || (bit t.regs.(r_crc_ctrl) 1 = 1 && hit_now) || fstart in
   509	      let c = crc_cfg t in
   510	      t.crc <- (if start then c.init else if cv then CRC.step c t.crc cb else t.crc);
   511	      if es_old.valid then begin
   512	        if t.mtime >= Array.length t.mhist then
   513	          t.mhist <- Array.append t.mhist (Array.make (Array.length t.mhist) 0);
   514	        t.mhist.(t.mtime) <- es_old.bit;
   515	        t.mtime <- t.mtime + 1
   516	      end;
   517	      (* the pin NCO (../multiphase/nco.ml): the levels of the accumulator at the clock's four
   518	         quarter points, registered; then the accumulator advances by inc *)
   519	      let inc = reg32 t r_nco_inc in
   520	      let a = [| t.nco_acc; u32 (t.nco_acc + (inc lsr 2)); u32 (t.nco_acc + (inc lsr 1));
   521	                 u32 (t.nco_acc + (inc lsr 1) + (inc lsr 2)) |] in
   522	      let l p = 1 - bit a.(p) 31 in
   523	      t.nco_out <- (l 0 lor (l 1 lsl 1) lor (l 2 lsl 2) lor (l 3 lsl 3))
   524	                   lor ((l 0 lor (l 0 lsl 1) lor (l 2 lsl 2) lor (l 2 lsl 3)) lsl 4)
   525	                   lor (rep4 (l 0) lsl 8);
   526	      t.nco_acc <- u32 (t.nco_acc + inc)
   527	    end;
   528	    (match act with
   529	     | Some (A_match b) -> for k = 36 downto 1 do t.chain.(k) <- t.chain.(k - 1) done; t.chain.(0) <- b
   530	     | _ -> ());
   531	    (* memories *)
   532	    (match act with
   533	     | Some (A_prog (a, b)) ->
   534	       if running then t.err_mem <- true
   535	       else if a land 1 = 0 then t.prog_lo <- b
   536	       else t.prog.((a lsr 1) land (t.cfg.prog_words - 1)) <- (b lsl 8) lor t.prog_lo
   537	     | Some (A_bank (a, b)) -> if running then t.err_mem <- true else t.bank.(a land (bank_bytes - 1)) <- b
   538	     | Some (A_stream (0, b)) -> t.str_lo <- b
   539	     | Some (A_stream (1, b)) -> t.str_hi <- b
   540	     | _ -> ());
   541	    (* the host link *)
   542	    (match fetched with Some (b, _) -> t.rbuf <- b | None -> ());
   543	    t.pend_fetch <- None;
   544	    let next_action = ref None in
   545	    let s = lvl pad_hstb and r = lvl pad_hrd in
   546	    let d = Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.init 4 (fun k -> lvl (pad_hdata + k) lsl k)) in
   547	    if r <> t.prev_r then begin
   548	      (* raising R starts reading what a READ command fetched; lowering it ends the command *)
   549	      t.nphase <- 0; t.rphase <- 0;
   550	      if r = 0 then t.pst <- Idle
   551	    end
   552	    else if s <> t.prev_s then begin
   553	      if r = 0 then begin
   554	        if t.nphase = 0 then (t.hi_nib <- d; t.nphase <- 1)
   555	        else begin
   556	          t.nphase <- 0;
   557	          let b = (t.hi_nib lsl 4) lor d in
   558	          match t.pst with
   559	          | Idle ->
   560	            let op = b lsr 4 in
   561	            if op = op_write || op = op_read then (t.op <- op; t.tgt <- b land 15; t.pst <- Addr_hi)
   562	          | Addr_hi -> t.addr <- b lsl 8; t.pst <- Addr_lo
   563	          | Addr_lo -> t.addr <- t.addr lor b; t.pst <- Count
   564	          | Count ->
   565	            t.remaining <- b + 1;
   566	            if t.op = op_write then t.pst <- Wdata
   567	            else (t.pst <- Rdata; t.pend_fetch <- Some (t.tgt, t.addr))
   568	          | Wdata ->
   569	            next_action := action_of ~tgt:t.tgt ~addr:t.addr b;
   570	            t.addr <- (t.addr + 1) land 0xFFFF;
   571	            t.remaining <- t.remaining - 1;
   572	            if t.remaining = 0 then t.pst <- Idle
   573	          | Rdata -> t.pst <- Idle
   574	        end
   575	      end
   576	      else if t.rphase = 0 then t.rphase <- 1
   577	      else begin
   578	        t.rphase <- 0;
   579	        if t.pst = Rdata && t.remaining > 1 then begin
   580	          t.remaining <- t.remaining - 1;
   581	          t.addr <- (t.addr + 1) land 0xFFFF;
   582	          t.pend_fetch <- Some (t.tgt, t.addr)
   583	        end
   584	        else begin
   585	          t.remaining <- 0; t.rbuf <- 0;
   586	          if t.pst = Rdata then t.pst <- Idle
   587	        end
   588	      end
   589	    end;
   590	    t.prev_s <- s; t.prev_r <- r;
   591	    (* an action waits a clock when a thread's SEND took the array's write port; a newer byte
   592	       overwrites a waiting one, and that is recorded *)
   593	    let waiting = match act with Some (A_peseg _) when not host_mbx_done -> hit "host feed write waited for a SEND"; act | _ -> None in
   594	    (match !next_action, waiting with
   595	     | Some a, Some _ -> t.act_drop <- true; t.pend_action <- Some a
   596	     | Some a, None -> t.pend_action <- Some a
   597	     | None, w -> t.pend_action <- w);
   598	    (* register writes land at the end of the clock *)
   599	    (match act with
   600	     | Some (A_reg (a, b)) ->
   601	       if a = r_status then (t.err_mem <- false; t.hin_ovf <- false; t.hout_ovf <- false; t.act_drop <- false)
   602	       else if a < reg_space && rw_bits a > 0 then t.regs.(a) <- b land ((1 lsl rw_bits a) - 1)
   603	     | _ -> ());
   604	    (* a clock spent in clear leaves the sequencer in its reset state, from the boot registers as
   605	       they were during the clock; a held streamer and sampler are recreated with the
   606	       configuration as it is now, which is what their idle outputs show in the next clock *)
   607	    if not running then t.seq <- Isa2.Spec.reset ~boot:boot_now ~bank:t.bank;
   608	    if hold then begin
   609	      t.str <- SM.create (streamer_cfg t);
   610	      t.smp <- PM.create (sampler_cfg t)
   611	    end;
   612	    out
   613	  end

    57	let config = Chip_spec.default_config

    59	(* A synchronous single-port memory: [addr], [we] and [wdata] in one clock; the word at [addr]
    60	   is on the output during the next clock. *)
    61	type mem = clock:Signal.t -> size:int -> width:int -> addr:Signal.t -> we:Signal.t -> wdata:Signal.t -> Signal.t
    62	
    63	let behavioural_mem : mem =
    64	 fun ~clock ~size ~width:_ ~addr ~we ~wdata ->
    65	  let abits = address_bits_for size in
    66	  let a = select addr (abits - 1) 0 in
    67	  let q = memory size ~write_port:{ write_clock = clock; write_address = a; write_enable = we; write_data = wdata }
    68	      ~read_address:(reg (Reg_spec.create ~clock ()) a) in
    69	  q
    70	
    71	type mems = { prog_mem : mem; bank_mem : mem }
    72	
    73	let behavioural = { prog_mem = behavioural_mem; bank_mem = behavioural_mem }
    74	
    75	type outputs = { pad_nib : Signal.t; uio_oe : Signal.t; dbg : (string * Signal.t) list }
    76	
    77	(* a FIFO of [depth] entries; a push into a full FIFO is taken when the head leaves in the same
    78	   clock. Returns head, count, accept. *)
    79	let fifo ~name spec ~depth ~push ~pop ~data =
    80	  let pbits = address_bits_for depth in
    81	  let ( -- ) s n = s -- (name ^ "_" ^ n) in
    82	  let rd = wire pbits -- "rd" and wr = wire pbits -- "wr" and count = wire (pbits + 1) -- "count" in
    83	  let full = count ==:. depth and nonempty = count <>:. 0 in
    84	  let popping = pop &: nonempty in
    85	  let accept = push &: (~:full |: popping) in
    86	  let slots = List.init depth (fun i -> reg spec ~enable:(accept &: (wr ==:. i)) data -- Printf.sprintf "slot%d" i) in
    87	  rd <== reg spec (mux2 popping (rd +:. 1) rd);
    88	  wr <== reg spec (mux2 accept (wr +:. 1) wr);
    89	  count <== reg spec (count +: uresize accept (pbits + 1) -: uresize popping (pbits + 1));
    90	  (mux rd slots, count, accept, full, nonempty)
    91	
    92	let onehot_index v = mux2 (bit v 0) (of_int ~width:2 0) (mux2 (bit v 1) (of_int ~width:2 1) (mux2 (bit v 2) (of_int ~width:2 2) (of_int ~width:2 3)))
    93	let rep4 b = repeat b 4
    94	let nib v i = select v (4 * i + 3) (4 * i)
    95	
    96	let create ?(cfg = config) ?(mems = behavioural) ~clock ~reset ~smp () =
    97	  let spec = Reg_spec.create ~clock ~clear:reset () in
    98	  let lvl i = bit smp (4 * i + 3) in
    99	  (* ---- the pending host action (decoded last clock), and the register file ---- *)
   100	  let act_valid = wire 1 and act_tgt = wire 4 and act_addr = wire 16 and act_byte = wire 8 in
   101	  let act_is tg = act_valid &: (act_tgt ==:. tg) &: ~:reset in
   102	  let act_reg_at a = act_is t_reg &: (act_addr ==:. a) in
   103	  let regs =
   104	    Array.init reg_space (fun a ->
   105	        let w = rw_bits a in
   106	        if w = 0 then zero 8
   107	        else
   108	          let rs = Reg_spec.override spec ~clear_to:(of_int ~width:8 (reset_value a)) in
   109	          let d = uresize (select act_byte (w - 1) 0) 8 in
   110	          reg rs ~enable:(act_reg_at a) d -- Printf.sprintf "cfgreg_%02x" a)
   111	  in
   112	  let reg32 a = concat_msb [ regs.(a + 3); regs.(a + 2); regs.(a + 1); regs.(a) ] in
   113	  let run = bit regs.(r_ctrl) 0 and hold = bit regs.(r_ctrl) 1 in
   114	  let pulse a = act_reg_at a in
   115	  (* ---- the sequencer ---- *)
   116	  let pin_sel k = select regs.(r_pinin k) 3 0 in
   117	  let pad_nib_in i = nib smp i in
   118	  let sel_pad sel = mux sel (List.init n_pads pad_nib_in) in
   119	  let pin_k k =
   120	    let k = if is "pinin_lane_swap" then (match k with 2 -> 3 | 3 -> 2 | k -> k) else k in
   121	    sel_pad (pin_sel k)
   122	  in
   123	  let pin_in = concat_lsb (List.init 8 (fun k -> bit (pin_k k) (if is "pinin_quarter0" then 0 else 3))) in
   124	  let pin_in4 = concat_lsb (List.init 8 (fun k -> pin_k k)) in
   125	  let imem_data = wire 16 and bank_rdata = wire 8 in
   126	  let hostin_head = wire 8 and hostin_nonempty = wire 1 in
   127	  let port_in = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> wire 8) and port_in_valid = wire 4 and port_out_ready = wire 4 in
   128	  let flags = wire 16 in
   129	  let boot_pcs = List.init 4 (fun t -> regs.(r_boot_pc t)) in
   130	  let boot_pcs = if is "boot_pc_swap" then (match boot_pcs with [ a; b; c; d ] -> [ a; c; b; d ] | l -> l) else boot_pcs in
   131	  let restart = pulse r_restart in
   132	  let seq =
   133	    Sequencer2.create ~clock ~clear:(reset |: ~:run) ~imem_data ~pin_in ~pin_in4 ~host_in:hostin_head
   134	      ~host_in_valid:hostin_nonempty ~port_in ~port_in_valid ~port_out_ready ~flags
   135	      ~ctl_valid:restart ~ctl_thread:(select act_byte 1 0)
   136	      ~ctl_page:(if is "restart_page" then zero 2 else select act_byte 3 2) ~ctl_pc:regs.(r_restart_pc)
   137	      ~boot_page:regs.(r_boot_page) ~boot_pc:(concat_lsb boot_pcs) ~bank_rdata ()
   138	  in
   139	  let gate s = if is "hostin_ungated" then s else s &: run in
   140	  let host_in_ready = gate seq.host_in_ready in
   141	  let port_in_ready = seq.port_in_ready &: repeat run 4 in
   142	  (* the core owns the bank port while it runs; in clear its strobes are combinational of the
   143	     fetched word and must not reach the bank (the planted bug lets them) *)
   144	  let core_owns_bank = if is "bank_ungated" then run |: seq.bank_we else run in
   145	  let core_bank_we = seq.bank_we &: core_owns_bank &: ~:reset in
   146	  (* ---- the host link: input side ---- *)
   147	  let s = lvl pad_hstb and rr = lvl pad_hrd in
   148	  let d = concat_lsb (List.init 4 (fun k -> lvl (pad_hdata + k))) in
   149	  let prev_s = reg (Reg_spec.create ~clock ()) s and prev_r = reg (Reg_spec.create ~clock ()) rr in
   150	  let open Always in
   151	  let nphase = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and hi_nib = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 in
   152	  let pst = Variable.reg spec ~width:3 in
   153	  let st_idle = 0 and st_ahi = 1 and st_alo = 2 and st_count = 3 and st_wdata = 4 and st_rdata = 5 in
   154	  let is_read = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and tgt = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 in
   155	  let addr = Variable.reg spec ~width:16 and remaining = Variable.reg spec ~width:9 in
   156	  let rbuf = Variable.reg spec ~width:8 and rphase = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   157	  let fetch_valid = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and fetch_tgt = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 and fetch_addr = Variable.reg spec ~width:16 in
   158	  let nf_valid = Variable.wire ~default:gnd and nf_addr = Variable.wire ~default:(zero 16) in
   159	  let na_valid = Variable.wire ~default:gnd and na_byte = Variable.wire ~default:(zero 8) in
   160	  let rbuf_zero = Variable.wire ~default:gnd in
   161	  let byte_in = if is "nibble_order" then concat_msb [ d; hi_nib.value ] else concat_msb [ hi_nib.value; d ] in
   162	  let s_edge = s ^: prev_s and r_edge = rr ^: prev_r in
   163	  compile
   164	    [ if_ r_edge
   165	        [ nphase <--. 0; rphase <--. 0; when_ (rr ==:. 0) [ pst <--. st_idle ] ]
   166	        [ when_ s_edge
   167	            [ if_ (rr ==:. 0)
   168	                [ if_ (nphase.value ==:. 0)
   169	                    [ hi_nib <-- d; nphase <--. 1 ]
   170	                    [ nphase <--. 0
   171	                    ; switch pst.value
   172	                        [ of_int ~width:3 st_idle,
   173	                          [ when_ ((select byte_in 7 4 ==:. op_write) |: (select byte_in 7 4 ==:. op_read))
   174	                              [ is_read <-- (select byte_in 7 4 ==:. op_read); tgt <-- select byte_in 3 0
   175	                              ; pst <--. st_ahi ] ]
   176	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_ahi, [ addr <-- concat_msb [ byte_in; zero 8 ]; pst <--. st_alo ]
   177	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_alo, [ addr <-- concat_msb [ select addr.value 15 8; byte_in ]; pst <--. st_count ]
   178	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_count,
   179	                          [ remaining <-- uresize byte_in 9 +:. 1
   180	                          ; if_ is_read.value [ pst <--. st_rdata; nf_valid <-- vdd; nf_addr <-- addr.value ]
   181	                              [ pst <--. st_wdata ] ]
   182	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_wdata,
   183	                          [ na_valid <-- vdd; na_byte <-- byte_in
   184	                          ; addr <-- addr.value +:. 1; remaining <-- remaining.value -:. 1
   185	                          ; when_ (remaining.value ==:. 1) [ pst <--. st_idle ] ]
   186	                        ; of_int ~width:3 st_rdata, [ pst <--. st_idle ] ] ] ]
   187	                [ if_ (rphase.value ==:. 0) [ rphase <--. 1 ]
   188	                    [ rphase <--. 0
   189	                    ; if_ ((pst.value ==:. st_rdata) &: (remaining.value >:. 1))
   190	                        [ remaining <-- remaining.value -:. 1; addr <-- addr.value +:. 1
   191	                        ; nf_valid <-- vdd; nf_addr <-- addr.value +:. 1 ]
   192	                        [ remaining <--. 0; rbuf_zero <-- vdd
   193	                        ; when_ (pst.value ==:. st_rdata) [ pst <--. st_idle ] ] ] ] ] ] ];
   194	  (* ---- fetches ---- *)
   195	  let next_fetch_tgt = tgt.value in
   196	  let fv = fetch_valid.value and ft = fetch_tgt.value and fa = fetch_addr.value in
   197	  let fetch_is tg = fv &: (ft ==:. tg) in
   198	  (* ---- the array and the ports ---- *)
   199	  let es_bit = wire 1 and es_valid = wire 1 in
   200	  let push_v = seq.port_out_valid and push_d = seq.port_out_data in
   201	  let thread_push = (push_v <>:. 0) &: ~:reset in
   202	  let push_port = onehot_index push_v in
   203	  let push_seg = if is "mbx_port_swap" then mux2 (push_port ==:. 1) (of_int ~width:2 2) push_port else push_port in
   204	  let send_hi = Array.init 4 (fun _ -> wire 1) in
   205	  let send_hi_sel = mux push_port (Array.to_list send_hi) in
   206	  let host_seg = act_is t_peseg in
   207	  let host_wins = if is "host_mbx_priority" then host_seg else gnd in
   208	  (* the bit path's port (r_bp_ctrl): its SENDs fill the serialiser, its RECVs read the framer *)
   209	  let bp_en = bit regs.(r_bp_ctrl) 0 and bp_port = select regs.(r_bp_ctrl) 2 1 in
   210	  let bp_port = if is "bp_port_next" then bp_port +:. 1 else bp_port in
   211	  let routed p = bp_en &: (bp_port ==:. p) in
   212	  let push_routed = mux push_port (List.init 4 routed) in
   213	  let tx_push = thread_push &: push_routed in
   214	  let thread_push = thread_push &: ~:push_routed in
   215	  let use_thread = thread_push &: ~:host_wins in
   216	  let mbx_wr = use_thread |: host_seg in
   217	  let mbx_seg = mux2 use_thread push_seg (select act_addr 3 2) in
   218	  let send_first_hi = if is "send_order" then ~:send_hi_sel else send_hi_sel in
   219	  let mbx_sel = mux2 use_thread (uresize send_first_hi 2) (select act_addr 1 0) in
   220	  let mbx_byte = mux2 use_thread push_d act_byte in
   221	  let host_mbx_done = host_seg &: ~:use_thread in
   222	  (* the chains' segment is address bits 9:8, so that a multi-byte write stays in its segment *)
   223	  let cfg_seg = if is "pecfg_seg" then select act_addr 10 9 else select act_addr 9 8 in
   224	  let fixed_seg = if is "fixed_port_seg1" then 1 else 0 in
   225	  let arr =
   226	    Upe.Upe_rtl.array_create ~layout:cfg.layout ?clear:(if is "array_unreset" then None else Some reset) ~clock
   227	      { mbx_wr; mbx_seg; mbx_sel; mbx_byte;
   228	        acfg_wr = act_is t_pecfg; acfg_seg = cfg_seg; acfg_byte = act_byte;
   229	        ainit_wr = act_is t_peinit; ainit_seg = select act_addr 9 8; ainit_byte = act_byte;
   230	        fixed_d = Array.init 4 (fun j -> if j = fixed_seg then uresize es_bit 16 else zero 16);
   231	        fixed_v = Array.init 4 (fun j -> if j = fixed_seg then es_valid else gnd) }
   232	  in
   233	  (* names for timing reports: the array's per-PE and per-segment state, and its g and step chains *)
   234	  Array.iteri (fun i (pe : Upe.Upe_rtl.pe_out) ->
   235	      let nm f x = ignore (x -- Printf.sprintf "pe%d_%s" i f) in
   236	      nm "s" pe.s; nm "p" pe.p; nm "pv" pe.pv; nm "f" pe.f; nm "l" pe.l; nm "g" pe.g; nm "step" pe.step;
   237	      nm "cfg" pe.cfg; nm "tap" pe.tap) arr.pes;
   238	  Array.iteri (fun j (r : Upe.Upe_rtl.seg_regs) ->
   239	      let nm f x = ignore (x -- Printf.sprintf "seg%d_%s" j f) in
   240	      nm "flo" r.flo; nm "fhi" r.fhi; nm "fv" r.fv0; nm "ctrl" r.ctrl; nm "rep" r.rep; nm "cnt" r.cnt; nm "fw" r.fw) arr.segs;
   241	  let port_reset = mux2 (pulse r_port_reset) (select act_byte 3 0) (zero 4) in
   242	  let holds =
   243	    Array.init 4 (fun p ->
   244	        let st = Variable.reg spec ~width:2 and w = Variable.reg spec ~width:16 in
   245	        ignore (st.value -- Printf.sprintf "hold%d_st" p); ignore (w.value -- Printf.sprintf "hold%d_w" p);
   246	        let src = if is "tap_seg_miswire" then (p + 1) mod 4 else p in
   247	        let pop = bit port_in_ready p &: (if is "rx_pop_hold_too" then vdd else ~:(routed p)) in
   248	        let hi = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   249	        ignore (hi.value -- Printf.sprintf "send_hi%d" p);
   250	        send_hi.(p) <== hi.value;
   251	        compile
   252	          [ when_ (thread_push &: (push_port ==:. p)) [ hi <-- ~:(hi.value) ]
   253	          ; if_ (bit port_reset p) [ hi <--. 0; st <--. 0 ]
   254	              [ switch st.value
   255	                  [ of_int ~width:2 0, [ when_ arr.tap_v.(src) [ st <--. 1; w <-- arr.tap_d.(src) ] ]
   256	                  ; of_int ~width:2 1, [ when_ pop [ st <--. 2 ] ]
   257	                  ; of_int ~width:2 2, [ when_ pop [ st <--. 0 ] ]
   258	                  ; of_int ~width:2 3, [] ] ] ];
   259	        (st.value, w.value))
   260	  in
   261	  let holds = if is "recv_port_swap" then Array.init 4 (fun p -> holds.(if p = 2 then 3 else p)) else holds in
   262	  let rx_head = wire 8 and rx_nonempty = wire 1 in
   263	  Array.iteri
   264	    (fun p (st, w) -> port_in.(p) <== mux2 (routed p) rx_head (mux2 (st ==:. 2) (select w 15 8) (select w 7 0)))
   265	    holds;
   266	  port_in_valid <== concat_lsb (List.init 4 (fun p -> mux2 (routed p) rx_nonempty (fst holds.(p) <>:. 0)));
   267	  let rx_pop = (port_in_ready &: concat_lsb (List.init 4 routed)) <>:. 0 in
   268	  (* ---- host FIFOs ---- *)
   269	  let hin_push = act_is t_hostin in
   270	  let hin_head, hin_count, hin_accept, hin_full, hin_ne =
   271	    if is "hostin_overwrite" then begin
   272	      let h, c, _, f, ne = fifo ~name:"hostin" spec ~depth:Chip_spec.hostin_depth ~push:hin_push ~pop:host_in_ready ~data:act_byte in
   273	      (h, c, hin_push, f, ne)
   274	    end
   275	    else fifo ~name:"hostin" spec ~depth:Chip_spec.hostin_depth ~push:hin_push ~pop:host_in_ready ~data:act_byte
   276	  in
   277	  hostin_head <== hin_head; hostin_nonempty <== hin_ne;
   278	  let hout_lat = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   279	  let hout_pop = wire 1 in
   280	  let hout_push = seq.host_out_valid in
   281	  let hout_head, hout_count, hout_accept, _, hout_ne =
   282	    fifo ~name:"hostout" spec ~depth:Chip_spec.hostout_depth ~push:hout_push ~pop:hout_pop ~data:(concat_msb [ seq.host_tag; seq.host_out ])
   283	  in
   284	  (* ---- assists ---- *)
   285	  let aclear = reset |: hold in
   286	  let str_lo = Variable.reg spec ~width:8 and str_hi = Variable.reg spec ~width:8 in
   287	  let str_out, str_oe, str_full =
   288	    Pstream.Streamer.create ~clock ~clear:aclear
   289	      ~period:(concat_msb [ select regs.(r_str_per + 1) 3 0; regs.(r_str_per) ])
   290	      ~width:(select regs.(r_str_per + 1) 6 4) ~od_mask:(select regs.(r_str_pins) 3 0)
   291	      ~idle_out:(select regs.(r_str_pins) 7 4) ~idle_oe:(select regs.(r_str_idle_oe) 3 0)
   292	      ~host_data:(concat_msb [ str_hi.value; str_lo.value ]) ~host_count:(select act_byte 3 0)
   293	      ~host_push:(act_is t_stream &: (select act_addr 1 0 ==:. 2)) ()
   294	  in
   295	  let smp_lat = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   296	  let smp_pop = wire 1 in
   297	  let smp_pins =
   298	    let pin j = bit (sel_pad (select regs.(r_smp_pin j) 3 0)) 3 in
   299	    let pins = List.init 4 pin in
   300	    let pins = if is "smp_pin_swap" then (match pins with a :: b :: rest -> b :: a :: rest | l -> l) else pins in
   301	    mux2 (bit regs.(r_smp_src) 0) (concat_lsb [ es_bit; es_valid; gnd; gnd ]) (concat_lsb pins)
   302	  in
   303	  let smp_data, smp_count, smp_valid, smp_overflows =
   304	    Psample.Sampler.create ~clock ~clear:aclear ~pins:smp_pins ~clocked:(bit regs.(r_smp_per + 1) 7)
   305	      ~period:(concat_msb [ select regs.(r_smp_per + 1) 3 0; regs.(r_smp_per) ])
   306	      ~width:(select regs.(r_smp_per + 1) 6 4) ~trig_pin:(select regs.(r_smp_off + 1) 5 4)
   307	      ~trig_val:(bit regs.(r_smp_off + 1) 6) ~offset:(concat_msb [ select regs.(r_smp_off + 1) 3 0; regs.(r_smp_off) ])
   308	      ~frame_len:regs.(r_smp_frame) ~pop:smp_pop ()
   309	  in
   310	  let es_cfg : Eth10.Edge_sampler.cfg_signals =
   311	    { s_mode = select regs.(r_es_mode) 1 0; s_invert = bit regs.(r_es_mode) 2;
   312	      s_holdoff = concat_msb [ select regs.(r_es_holdoff + 1) 1 0; regs.(r_es_holdoff) ];
   313	      s_timeout = concat_msb [ select regs.(r_es_timeout + 1) 1 0; regs.(r_es_timeout) ];
   314	      s_offset = regs.(r_es_offset); s_period = regs.(r_es_period) }
   315	  in
   316	  let es_pad = sel_pad (select regs.(r_es_pads) 3 0) in
   317	  let es_samples = if is "es_quarter_rev" then reverse es_pad else es_pad in
   318	  let es_or = if is "line_or_ignored" then gnd else bit regs.(r_es_mode) 4 &: bit es_pad 3 in
   319	  let es_active = ~:(bit regs.(r_es_mode) 3) |: bit (sel_pad (select regs.(r_es_pads) 7 4)) 3 |: es_or in
   320	  let es_b, es_v, es_end, _es_over, es_in_burst, es_held =
   321	    Eth10.Edge_sampler.create_with_level ~clock ~clear:aclear ~n:4 ~cfg:es_cfg ~samples:es_samples ~active:es_active
   322	  in
   323	  let es_b = es_b -- "es_bit_raw" and es_v = es_v -- "es_valid" and es_end = es_end -- "es_burst_end" in
   324	  let es_in_burst = es_in_burst -- "es_in_burst" in
   325	  (* the bit output is defined only while valid; hold the last one so that it is a level. The
   326	     block's own held-level register is that level (mux2 es_v es_b es_last, with es_last the level
   327	     a clock ago), without the multiplexer after the valid register's fan-out (README section 6) *)
   328	  let es_lvl = es_held -- "es_level" in
   329	  let es_last = reg (Reg_spec.override spec ~clear:aclear) es_lvl -- "es_last_reg" in
   330	  es_bit <== es_lvl; es_valid <== es_v;
   331	  let match_y, match_hit =
   332	    Eth10.Matcher_en.create ~clock ~clear:aclear ~enable:es_v ~x:es_b ~cfg_in:(bit act_byte 0)
   333	      ~cfg_shift:(act_is t_match)
   334	  in
   335	  let crc_cfg : Eth10.Crc_unit.cfg_signals =
   336	    { s_poly = reg32 r_crc_poly; s_init = reg32 r_crc_init; s_mask = reg32 r_crc_mask;
   337	      s_refl = bit regs.(r_crc_ctrl) 0; s_xorout = reg32 r_crc_xorout; s_residue = reg32 r_crc_residue }
   338	  in
   339	  (* ---- the bit-path assists: the transmit serialiser and the receive framer ---- *)
   340	  let bp_clear = if is "bp_unheld" then reset else aclear in
   341	  let code_sig v : Linecode.cfg_signals = { s_mode = select v 1 0; s_invert = bit v 2; s_toggle_on = bit v 3 } in
   342	  let stuff_sig v : Stuff.cfg_signals = { s_run = select v 3 0; s_either = bit v 4; s_value = bit v 5 } in
   343	  let tail_sig v : Txpath.tail_signals = { s_len = select v 3 0; s_la = bit v 4; s_lb = bit v 5 } in
   344	  let tx_cfg : Txpath.cfg_signals =
   345	    { s_period = concat_msb [ select regs.(r_tx_per + 1) 3 0; regs.(r_tx_per) ]; s_msb = bit regs.(r_tx_code) 4;
   346	      s_stuff = stuff_sig regs.(r_tx_stuff); s_code = code_sig regs.(r_tx_code);
   347	      s_tail1 = tail_sig regs.(r_tx_tail1); s_tail2 = tail_sig regs.(r_tx_tail2) } in
   348	  let txp = Txpath.create ~clock ~clear:bp_clear ~cfg:tx_cfg ~push:tx_push ~push_data:push_d in
   349	  (* a SEND to the bit path is ready when the FIFO will have room for it, counting one on its way *)
   350	  let pend = if is "tx_ready_no_pending" then gnd else tx_push in
   351	  let tx_ready = ~:hold &: ((uresize txp.count 3 +: uresize pend 3) <:. Txpath.depth) in
   352	  port_out_ready <== concat_lsb (List.init 4 (fun p -> mux2 (routed p) tx_ready vdd));
   353	  let ctrl = regs.(r_bp_ctrl) in
   354	  let rx_cfg : Rxpath.cfg_signals =
   355	    { s_code = code_sig regs.(r_rx_code); s_stuff = stuff_sig regs.(r_rx_stuff); s_msb = bit regs.(r_rx_code) 4;
   356	      s_pattern = regs.(r_rx_pattern); s_pmask = regs.(r_rx_pmask); s_start_on_pattern = bit ctrl 7;
   357	      s_half_duplex = bit ctrl 4; s_end_inactive = bit ctrl 5; s_end_burst = bit ctrl 6; s_skip = regs.(r_rx_skip) } in
   358	  let pulse_bits = mux2 (pulse r_bp_pulse) (select act_byte 1 0) (zero 2) in
   359	  let start_b, stop_b = if is "frame_pulse_swap" then (bit pulse_bits 1, bit pulse_bits 0) else (bit pulse_bits 0, bit pulse_bits 1) in
   360	  let rxp =
   361	    Rxpath.create ~clock ~clear:bp_clear ~cfg:rx_cfg ~bit:es_b ~valid:es_v ~active:es_active ~burst_end:es_end
   362	      ~start:start_b ~stop:stop_b ~tx_active:txp.active ~pop:rx_pop in
   363	  rx_head <== rxp.head; rx_nonempty <== rxp.nonempty;
   364	  let crc_frame = if is "crc_frame_ignored" then gnd else bit ctrl 3 in
   365	  let crc_bit = mux2 crc_frame rxp.crc_bit es_b and crc_valid = mux2 crc_frame rxp.crc_valid es_v in
   366	  let crc_start = pulse r_crc_start |: (bit regs.(r_crc_ctrl) 1 &: match_hit) |: (crc_frame &: rxp.frame_start) in
   367	  let crc_raw, _crc_value, crc_check =
   368	    Eth10.Crc_unit.create ~clock ~clear:aclear ~cfg:crc_cfg ~start:crc_start ~bit:crc_bit ~valid:crc_valid
   369	  in
   370	  let inc = reg32 r_nco_inc in
   371	  let inc = if is "nco_inc_bytes" then concat_msb [ select inc 31 16; select inc 7 0; select inc 15 8 ] else inc in
   372	  let nco = Mphase.Nco.create ~clock ~clear:aclear ~inc in
   373	  (* ---- flags ---- *)
   374	  let fsrc =
   375	    Array.to_list arr.tap_f @ Array.to_list arr.tap_v
   376	    @ [ es_v; es_lvl; es_in_burst; es_end; crc_check; match_hit; str_full; smp_valid; bit nco 0 ]
   377	    @ [ rxp.done_; rxp.err; rxp.nonempty; txp.active |: tx_push; es_active; ~:es_active; rxp.in_frame ]
   378	  in
   379	  let fsrc = fsrc @ List.init (32 - List.length fsrc) (fun _ -> gnd) in
   380	  let flag f = mux (select regs.(r_flagsel (if is "flag_off_by_one" then (f + 1) mod 16 else f)) 4 0) fsrc in
   381	  let fl = concat_lsb (List.init 16 flag) in
   382	  (* thread t's flags are bits 4t..4t+3; the planted bug hands it thread t+1's *)
   383	  flags <== (if is "flag_thread_rot" then concat_msb [ select fl 3 0; select fl 15 4 ] else fl);
   384	  (* ---- fetch execution ---- *)
   385	  let sticky_err = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and sticky_hin = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   386	  let sticky_hout = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and sticky_drop = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 in
   387	  let status =
   388	    concat_lsb [ sticky_err.value; sticky_hin.value; sticky_hout.value; hin_full; hout_ne; smp_valid; str_full;
   389	                 sticky_drop.value ] in
   390	  let read_reg a =
   391	    let cases =
   392	      List.filter_map
   393	        (fun x ->
   394	          let v =
   395	            if rw_bits x > 0 then Some regs.(x)
   396	            else if x = r_status then Some status
   397	            else if x = r_hostin_count then Some (uresize hin_count 8)
   398	            else if x = r_hostout_count then Some (uresize hout_count 8)
   399	            else if x >= r_crc_raw && x < r_crc_raw + 4 then Some (select crc_raw (8 * (x - r_crc_raw) + 7) (8 * (x - r_crc_raw)))
   400	            else if x = r_match then Some (concat_msb [ match_hit; zero 2; match_y ])
   401	            else if x = r_smp_overflows then Some smp_overflows
   402	            else if x = r_bp_status then
   403	              Some (concat_lsb [ rxp.in_frame; rxp.err; rxp.ovf; rxp.count; txp.busy; txp.count ])
   404	            else None
   405	          in
   406	          Option.map (fun v -> (x, v)) v)
   407	        (List.init reg_space Fun.id)
   408	    in
   409	    let in_space = (select a 15 8 ==:. 0) &: (select a 7 0 <:. reg_space) in
   410	    mux2 in_space (mux (select a 7 0) (List.init reg_space (fun x -> match List.assoc_opt x cases with Some v -> v | None -> zero 8))) (zero 8)
   411	  in
   412	  let prog_q = wire 16 and bank_q = wire 8 in
   413	  let fbyte =
   414	    mux ft
   415	      [ read_reg fa;
   416	        mux2 run (zero 8) (mux2 (bit fa 0) (select prog_q 15 8) (select prog_q 7 0));
   417	        mux2 run (zero 8) bank_q;
   418	        zero 8;
   419	        mux2 (bit fa 0)
   420	          (mux2 (if is "hostout_no_latch" then hout_ne else hout_lat.value) (select hout_head 7 0) (zero 8))
   421	          (mux2 hout_ne (concat_msb [ vdd; zero 4; select hout_head 10 8 ]) (zero 8));
   422	        zero 8; zero 8; zero 8; zero 8;
   423	        mux (select fa 1 0)
   424	          [ mux2 smp_valid (concat_msb [ vdd; zero 3; smp_count ]) (zero 8);
   425	            mux2 (smp_lat.value &: smp_valid) (select smp_data 7 0) (zero 8);
   426	            mux2 (smp_lat.value &: smp_valid) (select smp_data 15 8) (zero 8);
   427	            zero 8 ];
   428	        zero 8; zero 8; zero 8; zero 8; zero 8; zero 8 ]
   429	  in
   430	  hout_pop <== (fetch_is t_hostout &: bit fa 0 &: (if is "hostout_no_latch" then vdd else hout_lat.value));
   431	  smp_pop <== (fetch_is t_sample &: (select fa 1 0 ==:. 2) &: smp_lat.value &: ~:hold);
   432	  let mem_refused = run &: (fetch_is t_prog |: fetch_is t_bank |: act_is t_prog |: act_is t_bank) in
   433	  (* ---- memories ---- *)
   434	  let pwords = cfg.prog_words in
   435	  let host_prog_read = nf_valid.value &: (next_fetch_tgt ==:. t_prog) &: ~:run in
   436	  let host_prog_write = act_is t_prog &: ~:run &: bit act_addr 0 in
   437	  let prog_lo = Variable.reg spec ~width:8 in
   438	  let prog_word = if is "prog_byte_order" then concat_msb [ prog_lo.value; act_byte ] else concat_msb [ act_byte; prog_lo.value ] in
   439	  let prog_addr =
   440	    mux2 host_prog_write (srl act_addr 1) (mux2 host_prog_read (srl nf_addr.value 1) (uresize seq.imem_addr 16)) in
   441	  prog_q <== mems.prog_mem ~clock ~size:pwords ~width:16 ~addr:prog_addr ~we:host_prog_write ~wdata:prog_word;
   442	  imem_data <== prog_q;
   443	  let host_bank_read = nf_valid.value &: (next_fetch_tgt ==:. t_bank) &: ~:run in
   444	  let host_bank_write = act_is t_bank &: ~:run in
   445	  let bank_addr = mux2 core_owns_bank (uresize seq.bank_addr 16) (mux2 host_bank_write act_addr (mux2 host_bank_read nf_addr.value (zero 16))) in
   446	  bank_q <== mems.bank_mem ~clock ~size:Chip_spec.bank_bytes ~width:8 ~addr:bank_addr
   447	      ~we:(mux2 core_owns_bank core_bank_we host_bank_write) ~wdata:(mux2 core_owns_bank seq.bank_wdata act_byte);
   448	  bank_rdata <== bank_q;
   449	  (* ---- the pending action and fetch registers, and the glue registers ---- *)
   450	  let waiting = act_is t_peseg &: ~:host_mbx_done in
   451	  let a_valid = Variable.reg spec ~width:1 and a_tgt = Variable.reg spec ~width:4 in
   452	  let a_addr = Variable.reg spec ~width:16 and a_byte = Variable.reg spec ~width:8 in
   453	  act_valid <== (a_valid.value -- "act_valid"); act_tgt <== (a_tgt.value -- "act_tgt");
   454	  act_addr <== (a_addr.value -- "act_addr"); act_byte <== (a_byte.value -- "act_byte");
   455	  List.iter (fun (v, n) -> ignore (v -- n))
   456	    [ fetch_valid.value, "fetch_valid"; fetch_tgt.value, "fetch_tgt"; fetch_addr.value, "fetch_addr";
   457	      rbuf.value, "link_rbuf"; pst.value, "link_state"; addr.value, "link_addr"; remaining.value, "link_remaining";
   458	      tgt.value, "link_tgt"; hi_nib.value, "link_hi_nib"; prog_lo.value, "prog_lo"; hout_lat.value, "hout_lat";
   459	      smp_lat.value, "smp_lat"; sticky_err.value, "sticky_err"; seq.imem_addr, "seq_imem_addr";
   460	      seq.port_out_valid, "seq_port_out_valid"; seq.port_out_data, "seq_port_out_data"; seq.bank_addr, "seq_bank_addr";
   461	      seq.bank_we, "seq_bank_we"; seq.pin_sub, "seq_pin_sub"; seq.pin_oe, "seq_pin_oe"; flags, "flags";
   462	      prog_q, "prog_q"; bank_q, "bank_q"; nco, "nco"; crc_raw, "crc_raw" ];
   463	  let clear_sticky = pulse r_status &: (if is "status_not_cleared" then gnd else vdd) in
   464	  compile
   465	    [ if_ na_valid.value
   466	        [ a_valid <--. 1; a_tgt <-- tgt.value; a_addr <-- addr.value; a_byte <-- na_byte.value
   467	        ; when_ waiting [ sticky_drop <--. 1 ] ]
   468	        [ a_valid <-- waiting ]
   469	    ; fetch_valid <-- nf_valid.value; fetch_tgt <-- next_fetch_tgt; fetch_addr <-- nf_addr.value
   470	    ; if_ rbuf_zero.value [ rbuf <--. 0 ] [ when_ fv [ rbuf <-- fbyte ] ]
   471	    ; when_ (fetch_is t_hostout)
   472	        [ if_ (bit fa 0) [ hout_lat <--. 0 ] [ hout_lat <-- hout_ne ] ]
   473	    ; when_ (fetch_is t_sample)
   474	        [ when_ (select fa 1 0 ==:. 0) [ smp_lat <-- smp_valid ]
   475	        ; when_ (select fa 1 0 ==:. 2) [ smp_lat <--. 0 ] ]
   476	    ; when_ (act_is t_prog &: ~:run &: ~:(bit act_addr 0)) [ prog_lo <-- act_byte ]
   477	    ; when_ (act_is t_stream &: (select act_addr 1 0 ==:. 0)) [ str_lo <-- act_byte ]
   478	    ; when_ (act_is t_stream &: (select act_addr 1 0 ==:. 1)) [ str_hi <-- act_byte ]
   479	    ; if_ clear_sticky
   480	        [ sticky_err <--. 0; sticky_hin <--. 0; sticky_hout <--. 0; sticky_drop <--. 0 ]
   481	        [ when_ mem_refused [ sticky_err <--. 1 ]
   482	        ; when_ (hin_push &: ~:hin_accept) [ sticky_hin <--. 1 ]
   483	        ; when_ (hout_push &: ~:hout_accept) [ sticky_hout <--. 1 ] ] ];
   484	  (* ---- pads ---- *)
   485	  let taps_f = arr.tap_f and taps_d = arr.tap_d in
   486	  let source sel =
   487	    let seq_pin k = (nib seq.pin_sub k, bit seq.pin_oe k) in
   488	    let seq_pin k = if is "pin_lane_swap" then seq_pin (match k with 0 -> 1 | 1 -> 0 | k -> k) else seq_pin k in
   489	    let seq_pin k =
   490	      let n, oe = seq_pin k in
   491	      if is "quarter_swap" then (concat_lsb [ bit n 0; bit n 2; bit n 1; bit n 3 ], oe) else (n, oe)
   492	    in
   493	    let srcs =
   494	      List.init 8 seq_pin
   495	      @ List.init 4 (fun k -> (rep4 (bit str_out k), bit str_oe k))
   496	      @ [ (select nco 3 0, vdd); (select nco 7 4, vdd); (select nco 11 8, vdd); (zero 4, vdd) ]
   497	      @ List.init 4 (fun j -> (rep4 taps_f.(j), vdd))
   498	      @ List.init 4 (fun j -> (rep4 (lsb taps_d.(j)), vdd))
   499	      @ [ (rep4 es_lvl, vdd) ]
   500	      @ (let idle k =
   501	           let sel = if is "tx_idle_pin0" then zero 3 else select regs.(r_tx_idle) (4 * k + 2) (4 * k) in
   502	           let pins = List.init 8 seq_pin in
   503	           (mux sel (List.map fst pins), mux sel (List.map snd pins)) in
   504	         let line k l = (mux2 txp.busy (rep4 l) (fst (idle k)), mux2 txp.busy vdd (snd (idle k))) in
   505	         if is "tx_pads_swapped" then [ line 1 txp.b; line 0 txp.a ] else [ line 0 txp.a; line 1 txp.b ])
   506	    in
   507	    let srcs = srcs @ List.init (32 - List.length srcs) (fun _ -> (zero 4, vdd)) in
   508	    (mux sel (List.map fst srcs), mux sel (List.map snd srcs))
   509	  in
   510	  let rnib =
   511	    if is "read_nibble_order" then mux2 (rphase.value ==:. 0) (select rbuf.value 3 0) (select rbuf.value 7 4)
   512	    else mux2 (rphase.value ==:. 0) (select rbuf.value 7 4) (select rbuf.value 3 0)
   513	  in
   514	  let pad_out = Array.make n_pads (zero 4) and oes = Array.make 8 gnd in
   515	  List.iter
   516	    (fun p ->
   517	      let sel = regs.(r_padsel p) in
   518	      let n, oe = source (select sel 4 0) in
   519	      let n = mux2 (bit sel 5) (~:n) n in
   520	      if p < 8 then pad_out.(p) <- (mux2 oe n (rep4 (bit sel 6)) -- Printf.sprintf "pad%d_nib" p)
   521	      else (pad_out.(p) <- n; oes.(p - 8) <- oe))
   522	    general_out_pads;
   523	  for k = 0 to 3 do pad_out.(pad_hdata + k) <- rep4 (bit rnib k); oes.(k) <- prev_r done;
   524	  let oes = if is "uio_oe_swap" then (let o = Array.copy oes in o.(6) <- oes.(7); o.(7) <- oes.(6); o) else oes in
   525	  let dbg =
   526	    List.map (fun (n, s) -> ("seq_" ^ n, s)) seq.dbg
   527	    @ List.concat
   528	        (Array.to_list
   529	           (Array.mapi
   530	              (fun i (pe : Upe.Upe_rtl.pe_out) ->
   531	                [ Printf.sprintf "pe_s%d" i, pe.s; Printf.sprintf "pe_p%d" i, pe.p; Printf.sprintf "pe_pv%d" i, pe.pv;
   532	                  Printf.sprintf "pe_f%d" i, pe.f; Printf.sprintf "pe_l%d" i, pe.l; Printf.sprintf "pe_cfg%d" i, pe.cfg ])
   533	              arr.pes))
   534	    @ List.concat
   535	        (Array.to_list
   536	           (Array.mapi
   537	              (fun j (r : Upe.Upe_rtl.seg_regs) ->
   538	                [ Printf.sprintf "seg_flo%d" j, r.flo; Printf.sprintf "seg_fhi%d" j, r.fhi; Printf.sprintf "seg_fv%d" j, r.fv0;
   539	                  Printf.sprintf "seg_ctrl%d" j, r.ctrl; Printf.sprintf "seg_rep%d" j, r.rep;
   540	                  Printf.sprintf "seg_cnt%d" j, r.cnt; Printf.sprintf "seg_fw%d" j, r.fw ])
   541	              arr.segs))
   542	    @ [ "send_hi", concat_lsb (Array.to_list send_hi);
   543	        "holds", concat_lsb (Array.to_list (Array.map (fun (st, w) -> concat_msb [ w; st ]) holds));
   544	        "hin_count", hin_count; "hin_head", hin_head; "hout_head", hout_head;
   545	        "smp_head", concat_msb [ smp_valid; smp_count; smp_data ]; "str_out", concat_msb [ str_full; str_oe; str_out ]; "hout_count", hout_count; "crc", crc_raw; "nco", nco; "match_y", match_y;
   546	        "rbuf", rbuf.value; "es", concat_msb [ es_b &: es_v; es_v; es_end; es_in_burst ]; "es_last", es_last;
   547	        "tx", concat_msb [ txp.count; txp.busy; txp.active; txp.b; txp.a ]; "rx_head", rxp.head;
   548	        "rx", concat_msb [ rxp.count; rxp.ovf; rxp.err; rxp.in_frame ] ]
   549	    @ List.map (fun (n, s) -> ("bp_" ^ n, s)) rxp.dbg
   550	  in
   551	  { pad_nib = concat_lsb (Array.to_list pad_out); uio_oe = concat_lsb (Array.to_list oes); dbg }
   552	
   553	let circuit ?cfg ?mems ?(debug = true) () =
   554	  let clock = input "clock" 1 and reset = input "reset" 1 and smp = input "smp" (4 * n_pads) in
   555	  let o = create ?cfg ?mems ~clock ~reset ~smp () in
   556	  Circuit.create_exn ~name:"chip_core"
   557	    ([ output "pad_nib" o.pad_nib; output "uio_oe" o.uio_oe ]
   558	     @ if debug then List.map (fun (n, s) -> output n s) o.dbg else [])
