 (* The chip's host-visible encoding: host-link targets, the register map of target REG, the pad
    numbering and the source and flag codes. This is an encoding shared by the specification
    (chip_spec.ml), the RTL (chip_rtl.ml) and the host-side tools, as spec.ml is for the PE array;
    the behaviour behind it is written separately in each.
 
    Every register below is one byte at a byte address of target REG. Multi-byte fields are little
    endian. "rw" registers read back what was written; "w" registers are pulses and read 0; "r"
    registers are status. Reset value 0 unless a [reset] is given. *)
 
 (* host-link targets *)
 let t_reg = 0 and t_prog = 1 and t_bank = 2 and t_hostin = 3 and t_hostout = 4
 let t_pecfg = 5 and t_peinit = 6 and t_peseg = 7 and t_stream = 8 and t_sample = 9 and t_match = 10
 
 (* host-link command ops (the high nibble of a command byte) *)
 let op_write = 1 and op_read = 2
 
 (* pads: inputs 0-7 are ui_in, 8-15 uio_in; outputs 0-7 uo_out, 8-15 uio_out *)
 let n_pads = 16
 let pad_hdata = 8 (* 8..11: host data *) and pad_hstb = 12 and pad_hrd = 13
 let general_out_pads = [ 0; 1; 2; 3; 4; 5; 6; 7; 14; 15 ]
 let is_uio_general p = p = 14 || p = 15

 let src_tap_bit0 j = 20 + j  (* 20..23 *)

+                                         while idle, the sequencer pins r_tx_idle names.
+                                         27..31: constant 0 *)
 
 (* flag sources (5 bits) for the 16 flag inputs, thread t's flags are 4t..4t+3 *)

+   read; its error flag (a stuff or code violation in the frame); a byte is waiting; the transmit

 (* ---- the register map of target REG ---- *)
 let r_ctrl = 0x00            (* rw: bit 0 run, bit 1 hold the assists (streamer, sampler, edge
                                 sampler, CRC, matcher, NCO) in clear. reset: 2 *)
 let r_status = 0x01          (* r: bit 0 memory access refused (sticky), 1 HOSTIN overflow (sticky),
                                 2 HOSTOUT overflow (sticky), 3 HOSTIN full, 4 HOSTOUT non-empty,
                                 5 sampler FIFO non-empty, 6 streamer FIFO full;
                                 w: any write clears the sticky bits *)
 let r_hostin_count = 0x02    (* r *)
 let r_hostout_count = 0x03   (* r *)
 let r_boot_pc t = 0x04 + t   (* rw, t = 0..3 *)
 let r_boot_page = 0x08       (* rw: 2 bits per thread, thread 0 in bits 1:0 *)
 let r_restart = 0x09         (* w: thread [1:0], page [3:2]; pc from r_restart_pc: the core's
                                 host-control write (sequencer-v2 D1) *)
 let r_restart_pc = 0x0A      (* rw *)
 let r_port_reset = 0x0B      (* w: bit j empties port j's RECV holding register and resets its
                                 SEND byte order to "low byte next" *)
 let r_crc_start = 0x0C       (* w: loads the CRC register with its init value *)
 let r_padsel p = 0x10 + p    (* rw, pads 0..15 (only the general output pads are implemented):
                                 bits 4:0 source, bit 5 invert, bit 6 level when not driven.
                                 reset: pad k shows sequencer pin k; uio[6], uio[7] pins 6, 7 *)
 let r_pinin k = 0x20 + k     (* rw, logical pin k = 0..7: bits 3:0 input pad. reset: k *)
 let r_smp_pin j = 0x28 + j   (* rw, sampler pin j = 0..3: bits 3:0 input pad *)
 let r_smp_src = 0x2C         (* rw: bit 0: the sampler's pins are the recovered-bit stream
                                 {0, 0, valid, bit} instead of the pads *)
 let r_flagsel f = 0x30 + f   (* rw, flag f = 0..15: bits 4:0 flag source. reset: 31 (zero) *)
 let r_str_per = 0x40         (* rw, 2 bytes: bits 11:0 period; 0x41 bits 6:4 width (1, 2, 4) *)
 let r_str_pins = 0x42        (* rw: bits 3:0 open-drain mask, 7:4 idle level *)
 let r_str_idle_oe = 0x43     (* rw: bits 3:0 *)
 let r_smp_per = 0x44         (* rw, 2 bytes: bits 11:0 period; 0x45 bits 6:4 width, bit 7 clocked *)
 let r_smp_off = 0x46         (* rw, 2 bytes: bits 11:0 offset; 0x47 bits 5:4 trigger pin, bit 6
                                 trigger value *)
 let r_smp_frame = 0x48       (* rw: frame length *)

+                                invert, bit 3 use the active pad (else always active), bit 4 the

+(* ---- the bit-path assists (../bit-assists: Txpath, the transmit serialiser, and Rxpath, the
+   receive framer). Their configuration may change at any time (they read it live). ---- *)
+let r_bp_ctrl = 0x80         (* rw: bit 0 one MBX port is the bit path's: SEND on it fills the
+                                serialiser's FIFO and RECV reads the framer's; bits 2:1 that port
+                                (MBX channel 4 + p, segment p's port otherwise); bit 3 the CRC
+                                unit takes the framer's CRC feed and starts with each frame (else
+                                the edge sampler's bits, as without the assists); bit 4 half
+                                duplex (no frame starts while the serialiser is active); bit 5 a
+                                frame ends when the line is inactive; bit 6 at the edge sampler's
+                                burst end; bit 7 a frame starts on the pattern *)
+let r_rx_code = 0x81         (* rw: bits 1:0 decoder (0 pass, 1 NRZI, 2 Manchester), bit 2 invert,
+                                bit 3 NRZI toggles on 1 (else on 0, as USB), bit 4 MSB first *)
+let r_rx_stuff = 0x82        (* rw: bits 3:0 run (0: no destuffing), bit 4 either value, bit 5 the
+                                value a run is made of *)

+let r_rx_pmask = 0x84        (* rw: the pattern's bits that must match *)
+let r_rx_skip = 0x85         (* rw: frame bits before the CRC unit takes the frame's bits *)
+let r_tx_code = 0x86         (* rw: as r_rx_code, for the encoder *)

+let r_bp_pulse = 0x8D        (* w: bit 0 start a receive frame, bit 1 end it *)

 (* reset values of the rw registers that are not 0 *)
 let reset_value a =
   if a = r_ctrl then 2                         (* stopped, the assists held until configured *)
   else if a = r_str_per || a = r_smp_per then 1          (* period 1 *)
   else if a = r_str_per + 1 || a = r_smp_per + 1 then 0x10   (* width 1: the models need 1, 2 or 4 *)
   else if a >= r_padsel 0 && a <= r_padsel 7 then a - r_padsel 0     (* pad k shows sequencer pin k *)
   else if a = r_padsel 14 || a = r_padsel 15 then a - r_padsel 8      (* uio[6], uio[7]: pins 6, 7 *)
   else if a >= r_pinin 0 && a <= r_pinin 7 then a - r_pinin 0
   else if a >= r_flagsel 0 && a <= r_flagsel 15 then 31
   else 0
 
 (* Which addresses hold rw storage, and how many bits of the byte are kept. Writes to other
    addresses are ignored; reads of them return 0 (or status for the "r" registers). *)
 let rw_bits a =
   if a = r_ctrl then 2
   else if a >= r_boot_pc 0 && a <= r_boot_pc 3 then 8
   else if a = r_boot_page then 8
   else if a = r_restart_pc then 8
   else if a >= 0x10 && a <= 0x1F then (if List.mem (a - 0x10) general_out_pads then 7 else 0)
   else if a >= r_pinin 0 && a <= r_pinin 7 then 4
   else if a >= r_smp_pin 0 && a <= r_smp_pin 3 then 4
   else if a = r_smp_src then 1
   else if a >= r_flagsel 0 && a <= r_flagsel 15 then 5
   else if a = r_str_per then 8 else if a = r_str_per + 1 then 7
   else if a = r_str_pins then 8 else if a = r_str_idle_oe then 4
   else if a = r_smp_per then 8 else if a = r_smp_per + 1 then 8
   else if a = r_smp_off then 8 else if a = r_smp_off + 1 then 7
   else if a = r_smp_frame then 8
-  else if a = r_es_mode then 4 else if a = r_es_pads then 8
+  else if a = r_es_mode then 5 else if a = r_es_pads then 8
   else if a = r_es_holdoff || a = r_es_timeout then 8
   else if a = r_es_holdoff + 1 || a = r_es_timeout + 1 then 2
   else if a = r_es_offset || a = r_es_period then 8
   else if a = r_crc_ctrl then 2
   else if a >= r_crc_poly && a < r_crc_residue + 4 then 8
   else if a >= r_nco_inc && a < r_nco_inc + 4 then 8
+  else if a = r_bp_ctrl || a = r_rx_pattern || a = r_rx_pmask || a = r_rx_skip || a = r_tx_per then 8
+  else if a = r_rx_code || a = r_tx_code then 5
+  else if a = r_rx_stuff || a = r_tx_stuff || a = r_tx_tail1 || a = r_tx_tail2 then 6
+  else if a = r_tx_per + 1 then 4
+  else if a = r_tx_idle then 7
   else 0
 
 let rw_addresses = List.filter (fun a -> rw_bits a > 0) (List.init reg_space Fun.id)

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	(* A low-speed USB device (the HID boot keyboard of ../usb-ls) on the combined chip, with the
     2	   bit layer in the bit-path assists instead of firmware.
     3	
     4	   ../usb-ls ran the same device as firmware on a sequencer variant: T0, a whole thread, was the
     5	   bit layer (a software DPLL, NRZI, destuffing, EOP and reset detection, 63 words); T1 and T2
     6	   read bits one at a time and streamed replies precomputed as line symbols (sync, stuffing,
     7	   NRZI and EOP done by the controller); a 32-byte reply FIFO and patches to the running
     8	   programme carried the controller's decisions. Here the edge sampler (NRZ), the line decoder
     9	   (NRZI), the destuffer and the framer deliver bytes; the serialiser takes bytes and does sync
    10	   timing, stuffing, NRZI and the EOP; the CRC unit checks CRC-16 over the frame after the PID.
    11	   The firmware sees bytes only:
    12	
    13	   - T0, the protocol: receives each packet's PID and bytes from the framer (MBX port 0), checks
    14	     tokens by table lookup in the data bank (the address and endpoint byte and the CRC5 byte,
    15	     whose expected values the controller writes into two 256-byte tables when the address
    16	     changes), forwards SETUP and OUT data to the controller, checks the end of a packet through
    17	     flags (the frame's end, a stuff violation, the CRC residue), and answers: NAK, STALL or the
    18	     staged data packet to an IN; ACK or STALL after data. It sends the sync byte, the PID and the
    19	     bytes to the serialiser; the serialiser ends the packet with SE0, SE0, J when its FIFO runs
    20	     dry.
    21	   - T1, the mailbox: applies the controller's messages from HOSTIN to the data bank (the
    22	     replies, the per-endpoint choices, the tables).
    23	   - T2, bus reset: SE0 (the line-inactive flag) for more than [reset_slots] slots.
    24	   - T3 is free.
    25	
    26	   The chip is not allowed to have its programme store or bank written while it runs, so the
    27	   controller cannot patch the running programme as ../usb-ls's did; every decision it makes
    28	   reaches the chip as a message through HOSTIN, and T1 writes it into the bank. A reply the
    29	   controller staged before it saw a SETUP (or an OUT to endpoint 0, or a bus reset) would then be
    30	   stale: T0 (T2 for a reset) sets a "flush" byte when it reports such an event, T1 drops staged
    31	   writes while it is set, and the controller's first message after seeing the event clears it
    32	   and stages everything again. Table writes are never dropped. T0 itself withdraws a data reply
    33	   when the host ACKs it (the choice goes back to NAK before the event is reported), so the
    34	   controller's next message cannot race a repeat of the old data.
    35	
    36	   Data bank (page 0): 0 choice for IN on endpoint 0 (0 NAK, 1 STALL, 2 the staged data), 1 the
    37	   same for endpoint 1, 2 the handshake after OUT data on endpoint 0 (0 ACK, 1 STALL), 3 flush,
    38	   0x10 the staged packet's length n (PID, payload, CRC-16), 0x11.. its bytes. Page 1: the
    39	   class of a token's first byte (address and endpoint bit): 1 endpoint 0 of our address, 2
    40	   endpoint 1. Page 2: the class of its second byte (endpoint bits and CRC5) for the same.
    41	
    42	   Messages (HOSTIN): 0x01 a n b0 .. b(n-1): a staged write of n bytes at page 0, address a
    43	   (dropped while flush is set); 0x02 p a v: bank[p][a] <- v (a table entry); 0x03: clear flush.
    44	
    45	   Events (OUT with tag 1; forwarded bytes with tag 0), as ../usb-ls's where they mean the same. *)
    46	
    47	open Regs
    48	module I = Isa2

    50	let ev_reset = 1 and ev_ack0 = 2 and ev_ack1 = 3 and ev_setup = 4 and ev_out = 5 and ev_ok = 6 and ev_bad = 7
    51	let ev_out1 = 8 and ev_data = 9

    53	(* the bit path's MBX port: 4 + p, and the pads: uio[6] is D-, uio[7] is D+ (the open-drain

    59	(* ---- a small assembler with labels ---- *)

    61	type item = L of string | W of int | J of (int -> int) * string
    62	
    63	let assemble ~base items =
    64	  let labels = Hashtbl.create 64 in
    65	  let pc = ref base in
    66	  List.iter (function L l -> if Hashtbl.mem labels l then failwith ("label twice: " ^ l) else Hashtbl.replace labels l !pc | _ -> incr pc) items;
    67	  let addr l = match Hashtbl.find_opt labels l with Some a -> a | None -> failwith ("no label " ^ l) in
    68	  Array.of_list (List.filter_map (function L _ -> None | W w -> Some w | J (f, l) -> Some (f (addr l))) items)
    69	
    70	let fresh = let n = ref 0 in fun p -> incr n; Printf.sprintf "%s~%d" p !n
    71	let w x = W x
    72	let jmp l = J (I.jmp, l)
    73	let jnz l = J (I.jnz, l)
    74	let recv l = J ((fun a -> I.recv ~ch ~fail:a), l)
    75	let waitc f l = J ((fun a -> I.waitc ~cond:(I.c_flag f) ~fail:a), l)
    76	(* a SEND that waits while the serialiser's FIFO is full (with dl = 0 it jumps to itself) *)
    77	let send () = let l = fresh "send" in [ L l; J ((fun a -> I.send ~ch ~fail:a), l) ]
    78	let send_byte b = w (I.lda b) :: send ()
    79	let dec_cnt = w (I.shi ~pin:0 ~msb:0 ())   (* SHI on an unused pin: cnt - 1 (acc is clobbered) *)
    80	
    81	(* T0's flags (thread 0's flag inputs 0-3) *)
    82	let f_done = 0 and f_err = 1 and f_crc = 2 and f_active = 3
    83	
    84	type cfg = {
    85	  turn : int;          (* slots from the line's return to J to the reply's first byte *)
    86	  ack_wait : int;      (* slots to wait for the host's handshake after a data reply *)
    87	  check_crc : bool;    (* control: false accepts data without the CRC check *)
    88	  reset_slots : int;   (* SE0 longer than this is a bus reset *)
    89	}
    90	
    91	(* turn: the host releases the bus at the J after its SE0; the SOP must come 2 to 6.5 bit times
    92	   (80 to 260 clocks) after that edge. The flag sees the J 2 clocks after the pad (the stage),
    93	   T0 is within 4 clocks of it, then WAITD; the first byte reaches the serialiser 1 clock after
    94	   the SEND and its first symbol is on the pad 3 clocks later: about 4 turn + 10 clocks. *)
    95	let default_cfg = { turn = 24; ack_wait = 700; check_crc = true; reset_slots = 60 }
    96	
    97	let pid_in = 0x69 and pid_setup = 0x2D and pid_out = 0xE1 and pid_ack = 0xD2 and pid_nak = 0x5A and pid_stall = 0x1E

    99	let t0 c =
   100	  (* the two bytes after a token's PID: page 1 classifies the first, page 2 the second; both must
   101	     give the same endpoint, the packet must then end (SE0) without a third byte or a violation *)
   102	  let byte2 l cls ok =
   103	    [ L l; w (I.ldd 100); recv "idle"; w (I.bank 2); w I.ldb; w (I.skeq cls); jmp "drain";
   104	      w (I.ldd 40); waitc f_done "drain"; w (I.ldd 0); waitc f_err ok; jmp "idle" ] in
   105	  let token p ~ok0 ~ok1 =
   106	    [ L p; w (I.ldd 100); recv "idle"; w (I.bank 1); w I.ldb; w (I.skne 1); jmp (p ^ ".e0") ]
   107	    @ (match ok1 with Some _ -> [ w (I.skne 2); jmp (p ^ ".e1") ] | None -> [])
   108	    @ [ jmp "drain" ]
   109	    @ byte2 (p ^ ".e0") 1 ok0
   110	    @ (match ok1 with Some l -> byte2 (p ^ ".e1") 2 l | None -> []) in
   111	  (* the line back at J after the host's EOP, then the turnaround *)
   112	  let turnaround = [ w (I.ldd 40); waitc f_active "idle"; w (I.ldd c.turn); w I.waitd ] in
   113	  let in_ep k =
   114	    let ks = string_of_int k in
   115	    [ L ("in" ^ ks); w (I.lda k); w (I.bank 0); w I.ldb ] @ turnaround
   116	    @ [ w (I.skne 2); jmp ("data" ^ ks); w (I.skne 1); jmp "stall"; jmp "nak" ]
   117	    (* the staged data packet: sync, then n bytes from the bank *)
   118	    @ [ L ("data" ^ ks) ] @ send_byte 0x80
   119	    @ [ w (I.lda 0x10); w (I.bank 0); w I.ldb; w I.cnta; L ("dl" ^ ks); w I.ldb ] @ send ()
   120	    @ [ dec_cnt; jnz ("dl" ^ ks);
   121	        (* the host's handshake; another packet is handled as one *)
   122	        w (I.ldd c.ack_wait); recv "idle"; w (I.skeq pid_ack); jmp "dispatch";
   123	        w (I.lda k); w (I.bank 0); w (I.lda 0); w I.stb;
   124	        w (I.outi ~tag:1 (if k = 0 then ev_ack0 else ev_ack1)); jmp "idle" ] in
   125	  let hs label pid = [ L label ] @ turnaround @ send_byte 0x80 @ send_byte pid @ [ jmp "idle" ] in
   126	  let set a v = [ w (I.lda a); w (I.bank 0); w (I.lda v); w I.stb ] in
   127	  [ L "idle"; w (I.ldd 0);
   128	    L "wait"; recv "wait";
   129	    L "dispatch";
   130	    w (I.skne pid_in); jmp "in";
   131	    w (I.skne pid_setup); jmp "setup";
   132	    w (I.skne pid_out); jmp "out";
   133	    (* anything else (an ACK outside a wait, a stray data packet): drain it *)
   134	    L "drain"; w (I.ldd 0); recv "drain2"; jmp "drain";
   135	    L "drain2"; waitc f_done "drain"; jmp "idle" ]
   136	  @ token "in" ~ok0:"in0" ~ok1:(Some "in1")
   137	  @ token "setup" ~ok0:"setup0" ~ok1:None
   138	  @ token "out" ~ok0:"out0" ~ok1:(Some "out1")
   139	  @ in_ep 0 @ in_ep 1
   140	  @ [ L "nak" ] @ send_byte 0x80 @ send_byte pid_nak @ [ jmp "idle" ]
   141	  @ [ L "stall" ] @ send_byte 0x80 @ send_byte pid_stall @ [ jmp "idle" ]
   142	  (* SETUP and OUT: the token's kind in cnt (1 SETUP, 2 OUT 0, 3 OUT 1), then one data path *)
   143	  @ [ L "setup0"; w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_setup); w (I.lda 1); w I.cnta; jmp "data";
   144	      L "out0"; w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_out); w (I.lda 2); w I.cnta; jmp "data";
   145	      L "out1"; w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_out1); w (I.lda 3); w I.cnta; jmp "data";
   146	      (* the data packet's PID, then every byte to the controller until the frame ends *)
   147	      L "data"; w (I.ldd 400); recv "idle"; w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_data); w (I.out ());
   148	      L "fwd"; w (I.ldd 0); recv "fchk"; w (I.out ()); jmp "fwd";
   149	      L "fchk"; waitc f_done "fwd";
   150	      (* the packet has ended: a stuff violation or a bad CRC means silence *)
   151	      waitc f_err "nerr"; jmp "bad";
   152	      L "nerr" ]
   153	  @ (if c.check_crc then [ waitc f_crc "bad" ] else [])
   154	  @ [ w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_ok); dec_cnt; jnz "notsetup" ]
   155	  (* SETUP: a new control transfer, so a staged endpoint-0 reply is stale; ACK *)
   156	  @ set 0 0 @ set 3 1 @ [ jmp "hs_ack" ]
   157	  @ [ L "notsetup"; dec_cnt; jnz "hs_stall" ]   (* OUT to endpoint 1, which has no OUT direction *)
   158	  (* OUT to endpoint 0: a staged data reply is withdrawn (the transfer is over); the handshake
   159	     the controller staged (STALL while endpoint 0 is stalled) *)
   160	  @ [ w (I.lda 0); w (I.bank 0); w I.ldb; w (I.skeq 2); jmp "o0f" ] @ set 0 0
   161	  @ [ L "o0f" ] @ set 3 1 @ [ w (I.lda 2); w (I.bank 0); w I.ldb; w (I.skne 1); jmp "hs_stall"; jmp "hs_ack";
   162	      L "bad"; w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_bad); jmp "idle" ]
   163	  @ hs "hs_ack" pid_ack @ hs "hs_stall" pid_stall
   164	
   165	let t1 =
   166	  [ L "t1"; w I.in_; w (I.skne 1); jmp "staged"; w (I.skne 2); jmp "table"; w (I.skne 3); jmp "unflush"; jmp "t1";
   167	    L "staged"; w (I.lda 3); w (I.bank 0); w I.ldb; w (I.skeq 0); jmp "discard";
   168	    w I.in_; w (I.bank 0); w I.in_; w I.cnta;
   169	    L "sl"; w I.in_; w I.stb; dec_cnt; jnz "sl"; jmp "t1";
   170	    L "discard"; w I.in_; w I.in_; w I.cnta;
   171	    L "dl"; w I.in_; dec_cnt; jnz "dl"; jmp "t1";
   172	    L "table"; w I.in_; w (I.skne 1); jmp "tp1"; w I.in_; w (I.bank 2); w I.in_; w I.stb; jmp "t1";
   173	    L "tp1"; w I.in_; w (I.bank 1); w I.in_; w I.stb; jmp "t1";
   174	    L "unflush"; w (I.lda 3); w (I.bank 0); w (I.lda 0); w I.stb; jmp "t1" ]
   175	
   176	(* T2's flags (thread 2's flag inputs 8-11): 0 the line is inactive (SE0), 1 active *)
   177	let t2 c =
   178	  [ L "t2"; w (I.ldd 0);
   179	    L "w"; waitc 0 "w";
   180	    w (I.ldd c.reset_slots); waitc 1 "rst"; jmp "t2";
   181	    L "rst"; w (I.outi ~tag:1 ev_reset);
   182	    (* NAK on both endpoints, ACK after OUT, and flush until the controller has seen the reset *)
   183	    w (I.lda 0); w (I.bank 0); w (I.lda 0); w I.stb; w I.stb; w I.stb; w (I.lda 1); w I.stb;
   184	    w (I.ldd 0); L "rw"; waitc 1 "rw"; jmp "t2" ]
   185	
   186	let t3 = [ L "t3"; jmp "t3" ]
   187	
   188	(* where the threads live: pages 0 and 1, the 512-word store of the hardened chip *)
   189	let placement = [| (0, 0x00); (1, 0x00); (1, 0x80); (1, 0xF0) |]
   190	
   191	let images c =
   192	  let progs = [| t0 c; t1; t2 c; t3 |] in
   193	  Array.mapi (fun t items -> let page, base = placement.(t) in (page, base, assemble ~base items)) progs
   194	
   195	(* ---- the chip's configuration, through the host link while it is stopped ---- *)
   196	
   197	let addr_tables addr =
   198	  (* the token's two bytes for endpoints 0 and 1 at [addr] (fw_codec, ../usb-ls) *)
   199	  let a0, c0 = Fw_codec.token_tail ~addr ~ep:0 and a1, c1 = Fw_codec.token_tail ~addr ~ep:1 in
   200	  [ (1, a0, 1); (1, a1, 2); (2, c0, 1); (2, c1, 2) ]
   201	
   202	(* [jk_swap]: control, D+ and D- exchanged at the pads *)
   203	let configure ?(jk_swap = false) (h : Host.t) c =
   204	  let wr tgt addr data = Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt; addr; data }) in
   205	  Host.wreg h r_ctrl 2;
   206	  Array.iter (fun (page, base, words) ->
   207	      wr t_prog (2 * ((page lsl 8) lor base)) (List.concat_map (fun x -> [ x land 0xFF; x lsr 8 ]) (Array.to_list words)))
   208	    (images c);
   209	  wr t_reg (r_boot_pc 0) (Array.to_list (Array.map snd placement)
   210	                          @ [ Array.fold_left ( lor ) 0 (Array.mapi (fun t (p, _) -> p lsl (2 * t)) placement) ]);
   211	  (* the line: the serialiser's line a (idle high: J) on D-, line b on D+; released when idle
   212	     (sequencer pins 6 and 7, never driven) *)
   213	  let dm, dp = if jk_swap then (pad_dp, pad_dm) else (pad_dm, pad_dp) in
   214	  wr t_reg (r_padsel dm) [ src_tx_a ]; wr t_reg (r_padsel dp) [ src_tx_b ];
   215	  wr t_reg (r_flagsel 0) [ fl_rx_done; fl_rx_err; fl_crc_check; fl_line_active; 31; 31; 31; 31; fl_line_inactive; fl_line_active ];
   216	  (* the edge sampler: NRZ on D+ (J low), active while D+ or D- is high (SE0 is inactive); 160
   217	     sub-samples per bit, the sample in the middle; a timeout of 1023 never ends a burst *)
   218	  wr t_reg r_es_mode [ 2 lor 8 lor 16; (dm lsl 4) lor dp; 8; 0; 0xFF; 3; 80; 160 ];
   219	  (* CRC-16/USB, checked over the frame after the PID: residue 0xB001 *)
   220	  let le32 v = List.init 4 (fun k -> (v lsr (8 * k)) land 0xFF) in
   221	  wr t_reg r_crc_ctrl [ 1 ];
   222	  wr t_reg r_crc_poly (le32 0xA001 @ le32 0xFFFF @ le32 0xFFFF @ le32 0xFFFF @ le32 0xB001);
   223	  (* the bit path: port 0; CRC from the frame; half duplex; a frame ends at SE0 and starts after
   224	     the sync (0x80, newest bit in bit 7); NRZI both ways, stuffing after six 1s; 40 clocks per
   225	     bit; the EOP is two symbols of SE0, then one of J; idle shows pins 6 and 7, released *)
   226	  wr t_reg r_bp_ctrl
   227	    [ 1 lor (port lsl 1) lor 8 lor 16 lor 32 lor 128; 0x01; 0x26; 0x80; 0xFF; 8;
   228	      0x05; 0x26; 40; 0; 0x02; 0x11; 0x76 ];
   229	  (* the bank: NAK, NAK, ACK, no flush; the tables for address 0 *)
   230	  wr t_bank 0 [ 0; 0; 0; 0 ];
   231	  List.iter (fun (p, a, v) -> wr t_bank ((p lsl 8) lor a) [ v ]) (addr_tables 0);
   232	  Host.wreg h r_ctrl 1
   233	
   234	(* ---- the controller, on the host's side of the link ----
   235	
   236	   ../usb-ls's controller.ml, with its transport replaced: it keeps the device's USB state and
   237	   decides every reply, written from the USB and HID specifications separately from the test
   238	   host's reference device; its decisions go to the chip as messages instead of patches. *)
   239	
   240	type ep0 =
   241	  | Idle
   242	  | Data_in of { data : int list; pos : int; toggle : int; zlp : bool }
   243	  | Status_in of [ `Addr of int | `Config of int | `Plain ]
   244	  | Status_out
   245	  | Stalled
   246	
   247	type ctl = {
   248	  mutable addr : int;
   249	  mutable configured : bool;
   250	  mutable ep0 : ep0;
   251	  mutable ep1_toggle : int;
   252	  reports : int list Queue.t;
   253	  mutable staged : (int * int) option;     (* endpoint, payload length of the staged reply *)
   254	  mutable token : [ `None | `Setup | `Out0 | `Out1 ];
   255	  mutable rx : int list option;
   256	  mutable sent_choices : int list;        (* what the chip's choice bytes were last set to *)
   257	  outq : int Queue.t;                     (* message bytes for HOSTIN *)
   258	  mutable messages : int;
   259	  mutable events : int;
   260	}
   261	
   262	let create_ctl () =
   263	  { addr = 0; configured = false; ep0 = Idle; ep1_toggle = 0; reports = Queue.create (); staged = None; token = `None;
   264	    rx = None; sent_choices = [ 0; 0; 0 ]; outq = Queue.create (); messages = 0; events = 0 }
   265	
   266	let take n l = List.filteri (fun i _ -> i < n) l
   267	let drop n l = List.filteri (fun i _ -> i >= n) l
   268	
   269	let msg c l =
   270	  if Sys.getenv_opt "USB_DEBUG" <> None then Printf.printf "      message %s\n" (String.concat " " (List.map (Printf.sprintf "%02x") l));
   271	  c.messages <- c.messages + 1; List.iter (fun b -> Queue.push b c.outq) l
   272	let staged_write c a bytes = msg c ([ 0x01; a; List.length bytes ] @ bytes)
   273	
   274	let choices ?(force = false) c =
   275	  let ch ep = match c.staged with Some (e, _) when e = ep -> 2 | _ -> if ep = 0 && c.ep0 = Stalled then 1 else 0 in
   276	  let l = [ ch 0; ch 1; (if c.ep0 = Stalled then 1 else 0) ] in
   277	  if force || l <> c.sent_choices then (staged_write c 0 l; c.sent_choices <- l)
   278	
   279	let stage c ~ep ~pid payload =
   280	  let pkt = Fw_codec.data_bytes pid payload in
   281	  (* the buffer is shared: no endpoint may be answering from it while it is rewritten *)
   282	  if List.exists (( = ) 2) (take 2 c.sent_choices) then (c.staged <- None; choices c);
   283	  c.staged <- Some (ep, List.length payload);
   284	  staged_write c 0x10 (List.length pkt :: pkt);
   285	  choices c
   286	
   287	let schedule c =
   288	  (if c.staged = None then
   289	     match c.ep0 with
   290	     | Data_in { data; pos; toggle; _ } -> stage c ~ep:0 ~pid:(if toggle = 1 then 0x4B else 0xC3) (take 8 (drop pos data))
   291	     | Status_in _ -> stage c ~ep:0 ~pid:0x4B []
   292	     | _ ->
   293	       if c.configured && not (Queue.is_empty c.reports) then
   294	         stage c ~ep:1 ~pid:(if c.ep1_toggle = 1 then 0x4B else 0xC3) (Queue.peek c.reports));
   295	  choices c
   296	
   297	let set_tables c ~old_addr ~addr =
   298	  List.iter (fun (p, a, _) -> msg c [ 0x02; p; a; 0 ]) (addr_tables old_addr);
   299	  List.iter (fun (p, a, v) -> msg c [ 0x02; p; a; v ]) (addr_tables addr)
   300	
   301	(* after a flush: clear it, then stage everything again *)
   302	let restage c =
   303	  msg c [ 0x03 ];
   304	  (match c.staged with
   305	   | Some (ep, n) ->
   306	     let pid, payload =
   307	       match ep, c.ep0 with
   308	       | 0, Data_in { data; pos; toggle; _ } -> ((if toggle = 1 then 0x4B else 0xC3), take 8 (drop pos data))
   309	       | 0, _ -> (0x4B, [])
   310	       | _ -> ((if c.ep1_toggle = 1 then 0x4B else 0xC3), Queue.peek c.reports) in
   311	     ignore n; c.staged <- None; stage c ~ep ~pid payload
   312	   | None -> ());
   313	  choices ~force:true c
   314	
   315	let setup c s =
   316	  let b i = List.nth s i in
   317	  let wlen = b 6 lor (b 7 lsl 8) in
   318	  let desc = match b 0, b 1, b 3 with
   319	    | 0x80, 0x06, 1 -> Some Descriptors.device
   320	    | 0x80, 0x06, 2 -> Some Descriptors.config
   321	    | 0x81, 0x06, 0x22 -> Some Descriptors.report
   322	    | _ -> None in
   323	  c.ep0 <-
   324	    (match desc with
   325	     | Some d -> let data = take wlen d in Data_in { data; pos = 0; toggle = 1; zlp = List.length data < wlen && List.length data mod 8 = 0 }
   326	     | None -> match b 0, b 1 with
   327	       | 0x00, 0x05 -> Status_in (`Addr (b 2 land 0x7F))
   328	       | 0x00, 0x09 -> Status_in (`Config (b 2))
   329	       | 0x21, 0x0A -> Status_in `Plain
   330	       | _ -> Stalled)
   331	
   332	(* the host ACKed the staged reply of endpoint [ep]; T0 has set its choice back to NAK *)
   333	let acked c ep =
   334	  c.sent_choices <- List.mapi (fun i v -> if i = ep then 0 else v) c.sent_choices;
   335	  match c.staged with
   336	  | Some (e, len) when e = ep ->
   337	    c.staged <- None;
   338	    if ep = 0 then
   339	      (match c.ep0 with
   340	       | Data_in r ->
   341	         let pos = r.pos + len in
   342	         if pos < List.length r.data then c.ep0 <- Data_in { r with pos; toggle = 1 - r.toggle }
   343	         else if len = 8 && r.zlp then c.ep0 <- Data_in { r with pos; toggle = 1 - r.toggle; zlp = false }
   344	         else c.ep0 <- Status_out
   345	       | Status_in a ->
   346	         (match a with
   347	          | `Addr n -> set_tables c ~old_addr:c.addr ~addr:n; c.addr <- n
   348	          | `Config n -> c.configured <- n <> 0; c.ep1_toggle <- 0
   349	          | `Plain -> ());
   350	         c.ep0 <- Idle
   351	       | _ -> ())
   352	    else (ignore (Queue.pop c.reports); c.ep1_toggle <- 1 - c.ep1_toggle)
   353	  | _ -> ()
   354	
   355	let debug = Sys.getenv_opt "USB_DEBUG" <> None
   356	let now = ref 0
   357	
   358	let handle c (tag, v) =
   359	  if debug then Printf.printf "      [%d] event %s %02x\n" !now (if tag = 0 then "byte" else "tag1") v;
   360	  if tag = 0 then (match c.rx with Some l -> c.rx <- Some (l @ [ v ]) | None -> ())
   361	  else begin
   362	    c.events <- c.events + 1;
   363	    if v = ev_reset then begin
   364	      set_tables c ~old_addr:c.addr ~addr:0;
   365	      c.addr <- 0; c.configured <- false; c.ep0 <- Idle; c.ep1_toggle <- 0; c.staged <- None; c.rx <- None; c.token <- `None;
   366	      c.sent_choices <- [ 0; 0; 0 ];
   367	      restage c
   368	    end
   369	    else if v = ev_ack0 then acked c 0
   370	    else if v = ev_ack1 then acked c 1
   371	    else if v = ev_setup then c.token <- `Setup
   372	    else if v = ev_out then c.token <- `Out0
   373	    else if v = ev_out1 then c.token <- `Out1
   374	    else if v = ev_data then c.rx <- Some []
   375	    else if v = ev_bad then c.rx <- None
   376	    else if v = ev_ok then begin
   377	      (match c.token, c.rx with
   378	       | `Setup, rx ->
   379	         (* T0 has withdrawn endpoint 0's reply and set flush; a staged endpoint-1 report is
   380	            withdrawn too (it is staged again when endpoint 0 has nothing to send) *)
   381	         c.staged <- None; c.sent_choices <- [ 0; List.nth c.sent_choices 1; List.nth c.sent_choices 2 ];
   382	         (match rx with
   383	          | Some (0xC3 :: l) when List.length l = 10 && Fw_codec.crc16 (take 8 l) = (List.nth l 8 lor (List.nth l 9 lsl 8)) -> setup c (take 8 l)
   384	          | _ -> c.ep0 <- Stalled);
   385	         restage c
   386	       | `Out0, _ ->
   387	         (match c.ep0 with
   388	          | Status_out | Data_in _ -> c.ep0 <- Idle; (match c.staged with Some (0, _) -> c.staged <- None | _ -> ())
   389	          | _ -> ());
   390	         restage c
   391	       | _ -> ());
   392	      c.rx <- None; c.token <- `None
   393	    end;
   394	    schedule c
   395	  end
   396	
   397	let offer_report c r = Queue.push r c.reports; schedule c
   398	
   399	(* ---- the host's side of the link: messages out, events in ---- *)
   400	
   401	(* One step of the host program, whenever the link is idle: send what fits in HOSTIN (its
   402	   count read first), else poll HOSTOUT, four entries at a time. *)
   403	let pump c (h : Host.t) =
   404	  if Host.idle h then begin
   405	    if not (Queue.is_empty c.outq) then
   406	      Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_reg; addr = r_hostin_count; n = 1; k = (fun l ->
   407	          let room = Chip_spec.hostin_depth - List.hd l in
   408	          let k = min room (min 12 (Queue.length c.outq)) in
   409	          if k > 0 then Host.submit h (Host.Write { tgt = t_hostin; addr = 0; data = List.init k (fun _ -> Queue.pop c.outq) })) })
   410	    else
   411	      Host.submit h (Host.Read { tgt = t_hostout; addr = 0; n = 8; k = (fun l ->
   412	          let rec go = function
   413	            | t :: d :: rest -> if t land 0x80 <> 0 then handle c (t land 7, d); go rest
   414	            | _ -> () in
   415	          go l) })
   416	  end

     1	(* The transmit serialiser: bytes in, a coded line pair out. A two-byte FIFO (filled by a thread's
     2	   SEND on the chip), a symbol timer, the stuff tracker (transmit) and the line coder (encoder),
     3	   then a two-phase tail. Protocol-agnostic; the protocols are configurations:
     4	
     5	   - USB low speed: 40 clocks per symbol, LSB first, stuff run 6 of ones, NRZI toggling on 0 with
     6	     the idle level J (inverted so that line a, D-, idles high); tail SE0 for 2 symbols, then J for
     7	     1, then released. The thread sends the sync byte 0x80, the PID and the rest.
     8	   - 10BASE-T: 3 clocks per symbol (half a bit), LSB first, no stuffing, Manchester; tail TD+ high
     9	     for 6 symbols (TP_IDL, 300 ns), then released to the idle level. The thread sends the
    10	     preamble, the SFD and the frame with its FCS.
    11	   - CAN, HDLC: pass or NRZI, stuff run 5; MSB first for CAN.
    12	
    13	   A frame starts when a byte is in the FIFO while the serialiser is idle: the clock after, the
    14	   stuff tracker and the encoder restart and the first symbol goes out (it is on [a], [b] from the
    15	   clock after that); then one symbol every [period] clocks. At every bit boundary the next bit is
    16	   a stuff bit if one is due, else the next data bit, from the shift register or from the FIFO's
    17	   head; if there is none, the frame's data has ended (an underrun ends the frame: the sender keeps
    18	   the FIFO from running dry until its last byte). A stuff bit due after the last data bit is
    19	   sent. Then the tail: [tail1] symbols of the level pair (a1, b1), then [tail2] of (a2, b2), each
    20	   0 to 15 symbols, and the serialiser is idle again. During data, b is the complement of a (a
    21	   differential pair); [busy] is high from the first symbol to the end of the tail.
    22	
    23	   The model below and the RTL further down are written separately from these rules. *)

    63	let first_bit c byte = if c.msb_first then (byte lsr 7) land 1 else byte land 1
    64	let shift c byte = if c.msb_first then (byte lsl 1) land 0xFF else byte lsr 1

    66	(* One clock. [push]: a byte offered to the FIFO at the end of this clock; returns whether it was
    67	   taken (the FIFO has room after this clock's pop). *)
    68	let step c st ~push =
    69	  let popped = ref false in
    70	  let enter_tail k =
    71	    let t = if k = 1 then c.tail1 else c.tail2 in
    72	    if k = 1 && t.len > 0 then (st.phase <- Tail1; st.tl <- t.len - 1; st.a <- t.la; st.b <- t.lb; st.busy <- true)
    73	    else if c.tail2.len > 0 then
    74	      (st.phase <- Tail2; st.tl <- c.tail2.len - 1; st.a <- c.tail2.la; st.b <- c.tail2.lb; st.busy <- true)
    75	    else (st.phase <- Idle; st.busy <- false)
    76	  in
    77	  let symbol () =
    78	    match st.phase with
    79	    | Idle -> ()
    80	    | Data ->
    81	      if Linecode.need_bit c.code st.enc then begin

    84	          else match Queue.peek_opt st.fifo with Some x -> Some (first_bit c x) | None -> None in
    85	        let x = Stuff.tx_peek c.stuff st.stf ~data in

    87	        match x with

    94	           | _ -> ());

    96	          st.a <- sym; st.b <- 1 - sym; st.busy <- true

    99	        let sym = Linecode.enc_tick c.code st.enc ~bit:0 in
   100	        st.a <- sym; st.b <- 1 - sym; st.busy <- true
   101	      end
   102	    | Tail1 -> if st.tl > 0 then st.tl <- st.tl - 1 else enter_tail 2
   103	    | Tail2 -> if st.tl > 0 then st.tl <- st.tl - 1 else (st.phase <- Idle; st.busy <- false)
   104	  in
   105	  (match st.phase with
   106	   | Idle ->
   107	     (* the stuff tracker and the encoder are held in restart while idle *)
   108	     Stuff.restart st.stf; Linecode.enc_restart st.enc; st.nb <- 0;
   109	     if not (Queue.is_empty st.fifo) then (st.phase <- Data; st.ctr <- 0)
   110	   | _ ->

   112	  ignore !popped;
   113	  (* the FIFO takes a push when it has room after this clock's pop *)

   124	type cfg_signals = {
   125	  s_period : Signal.t;   (* 12 bits *)
   126	  s_msb : Signal.t;

   137	    s_code = Linecode.cfg_const c.code; s_tail1 = tail_const c.tail1; s_tail2 = tail_const c.tail2 }

   150	type out = { a : Signal.t; b : Signal.t; busy : Signal.t; active : Signal.t; count : Signal.t; accept : Signal.t }
   151	
   152	let p_idle = 0 and p_data = 1 and p_tail1 = 2 and p_tail2 = 3
   153	
   154	(* [push], [push_data]: a byte offered this clock; [accept] says it was taken. *)
   155	let create ~clock ~clear ~(cfg : cfg_signals) ~push ~push_data =
   156	  let spec = Reg_spec.create ~clock ~clear () in
   157	  let phase = wire 2 and ctr = wire 12 and sh = wire 8 and nb = wire 3 and tl = wire 4 in
   158	  let a = wire 1 and b = wire 1 and busy = wire 1 in
   159	  (* the FIFO: two slots, read pointer, count *)
   160	  let count = wire 2 and rd = wire 1 in
   161	  let slot0 = wire 8 and slot1 = wire 8 in
   162	  let head = mux2 rd slot1 slot0 in
   163	  let fifo_ne = count <>:. 0 in
   164	  let is_idle = phase ==:. p_idle and is_data = phase ==:. p_data in
   165	  let tick = ~:is_idle &: (ctr ==:. 0) in
   166	  let first_bit x = mux2 cfg.s_msb (msb x) (if is "tx_first_bit_msb" then msb x else lsb x) in
   167	  let shifted x = mux2 cfg.s_msb (sll x 1) (srl x 1) in
   168	  let have_sh = nb <>:. 0 in
   169	  let data_valid = have_sh |: fifo_ne in
   170	  let data_bit = mux2 have_sh (first_bit sh) (first_bit head) in
   171	  let need = wire 1 in
   172	  let due = tick &: is_data &: need in
   173	  let stx = Stuff.tx ~clock ~clear ~restart:is_idle ~cfg:cfg.s_stuff ~due ~data_bit ~data_valid in
   174	  let underrun = if is "tx_no_final_stuff" then due &: ~:data_valid else stx.underrun in
   175	  let take = stx.take in
   176	  let enc_tick = tick &: is_data &: ~:underrun in
   177	  let enc = Linecode.encoder ~clock ~clear ~restart:is_idle ~cfg:cfg.s_code ~tick:enc_tick ~bit:stx.out_bit in
   178	  need <== enc.need_bit;
   179	  (* the symbol of this tick (the encoder's own line register holds it too, but [a] also carries
   180	     the tail's levels) *)
   181	  let sym = enc.sym in
   182	  let pop = take &: ~:have_sh in
   183	  (* tail sequencing at a tick *)
   184	  let t1 = cfg.s_tail1 and t2 = cfg.s_tail2 in
   185	  let t1_len = if is "tx_tail_skips_first" then mux2 (t1.s_len ==:. 0) t1.s_len (t1.s_len -:. 1) else t1.s_len in
   186	  let enter_tail2 = (* from data or tail 1 *)
   187	    [ (t2.s_len <>:. 0), (of_int ~width:2 p_tail2, t2.s_len -:. 1, t2.s_la, t2.s_lb, vdd) ] in
   188	  let to_idle = (of_int ~width:2 p_idle, zero 4, a, b, gnd) in
   189	  let pick l default = List.fold_right (fun (c, (p, t, la, lb, bz)) (p', t', la', lb', bz') ->
   190	      (mux2 c p p', mux2 c t t', mux2 c la la', mux2 c lb lb', mux2 c bz bz')) l default in
   191	  let from_underrun =
   192	    pick ((t1_len <>:. 0, (of_int ~width:2 p_tail1, t1_len -:. 1, t1.s_la, t1.s_lb, vdd)) :: enter_tail2) to_idle in
   193	  let from_tail1 = pick ((tl <>:. 0, (phase, tl -:. 1, a, b, busy)) :: enter_tail2) to_idle in
   194	  let from_tail2 = pick [ (tl <>:. 0, (phase, tl -:. 1, a, b, busy)) ] to_idle in
   195	  let sym_b = if is "tx_pair_not_complement" then sym else ~:sym in
   196	  let from_data =
   197	    pick [ (underrun, from_underrun) ] (phase, tl, sym, sym_b, vdd) in
   198	  let at_tick =
   199	    pick [ (is_data, from_data); (phase ==:. p_tail1, from_tail1) ] from_tail2 in
   200	  let n_phase, n_tl, n_a, n_b, n_busy = at_tick in
   201	  let n_phase = mux2 is_idle (mux2 fifo_ne (of_int ~width:2 p_data) phase) (mux2 tick n_phase phase) in
   202	  phase <== reg spec n_phase;
   203	  tl <== reg spec (mux2 tick n_tl tl);
   204	  a <== reg spec (mux2 tick n_a a);
   205	  b <== reg spec (mux2 tick n_b b);
   206	  busy <== reg spec (mux2 tick n_busy busy);
   207	  let reload = mux2 (cfg.s_period ==:. 0) (zero 12) (cfg.s_period -:. 1) in
   208	  let reload = if is "tx_period_off_by_one" then mux2 (cfg.s_period ==:. 0) (zero 12) cfg.s_period else reload in
   209	  ctr <== reg spec (mux2 is_idle (zero 12) (mux2 tick reload (ctr -:. 1)));
   210	  (* the shift register *)
   211	  let after_take = mux2 have_sh (shifted sh) (shifted head) in
   212	  sh <== reg spec (mux2 take after_take sh);
   213	  nb <== reg spec (mux2 is_idle (zero 3) (mux2 take (mux2 have_sh (nb -:. 1) (of_int ~width:3 7)) nb));
   214	  (* FIFO *)
   215	  let accept = push &: ((count <>:. depth) |: pop) in
   216	  let accept = if is "tx_fifo_full_accepts" then push else accept in
   217	  let wr = wire 1 in
   218	  let wr_slot = mux2 (accept &: (count ==:. depth) &: ~:pop) (~:wr) wr in
   219	  (* the overwrite fault writes the slot behind the write pointer *)
   220	  slot0 <== reg spec (mux2 (accept &: (wr_slot ==:. 0)) push_data slot0);
   221	  slot1 <== reg spec (mux2 (accept &: (wr_slot ==:. 1)) push_data slot1);
   222	  wr <== reg spec (mux2 (accept &: ~:((count ==:. depth) &: ~:pop)) (~:wr) wr);
   223	  rd <== reg spec (mux2 pop (~:rd) rd);
   224	  let n_count = count +: uresize (accept &: ~:((count ==:. depth) &: ~:pop)) 2 -: uresize pop 2 in
   225	  count <== reg spec n_count;
   226	  { a; b; busy; active = ~:is_idle |: fifo_ne; count; accept }
